    96→func TestDiscordSender_BodyTruncation(t *testing.T) {
    97→	var received []byte
    98→	srv := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    99→		received, _ = io.ReadAll(r.Body)
   100→		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
   101→	}))
   102→	defer srv.Close()
   103→
   104→	// Create a body longer than Discord's embed description limit (4096 chars).
   105→	longBody := make([]byte, 5000)
   106→	for i := range longBody {
   107→		longBody[i] = 'a'
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	sender := &DiscordSender{Client: srv.Client()}
   111→	ch := &models.NotificationChannel{
   112→		Type:   "discord",
   113→		Config: json.RawMessage(`{"webhook_url": "` + srv.URL + `"}`),
   114→	}
   115→	msg := Notification{
   116→		Title:   "test",
   117→		Body:    string(longBody),
   118→		Version: "v1.0.0",
   119→	}
   120→
   121→	err := sender.Send(context.Background(), ch, msg)
   122→	if err != nil {
   123→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	// Verify the body was truncated in the embed description.
   127→	var payload map[string]interface{}
   128→	if err := json.Unmarshal(received, &payload); err != nil {
   129→		t.Fatalf("received invalid JSON: %v", err)
   130→	}
   131→	embeds := payload["embeds"].([]interface{})
   132→	embed := embeds[0].(map[string]interface{})
   133→	desc := embed["description"].(string)
   134→	if len(desc) > 4096 {
   135→		t.Errorf("expected description to be at most 4096 chars, got %d", len(desc))
   136→	}
   137→}

    45→func (m *mockOrchestratorStore) GetProject(_ context.Context, id string) (*models.Project, error) {
    46→	if m.err != nil {
    47→		return nil, m.err
    48→	}

    55→func (m *mockOrchestratorStore) GetAgentRun(_ context.Context, id string) (*models.AgentRun, error) {

    61→	}

    70→}

   117→// --- mock sender for testing ---

   119→type mockNotifySender struct {

   124→
   125→func (m *mockNotifySender) Send(_ context.Context, _ *models.NotificationChannel, msg routing.Notification) error {
   126→	if m.sendErr != nil {
   127→		return m.sendErr
   128→	}

   141→// --- tests ---
   142→
   143→func TestSendProjectNotifications_WithSubscriptions(t *testing.T) {
   144→	projectID := "proj-1"
   145→	channelID := "ch-1"
   146→
   147→	store := &mockOrchestratorStore{
   148→		project: &models.Project{ID: projectID, Name: "My Project"},
   149→		projectSubscriptions: []models.Subscription{
   150→			{ID: "sub-1", ChannelID: channelID, Type: "semantic_release", ProjectID: &projectID},
   151→		},
   152→		channels: map[string]*models.NotificationChannel{
   153→			channelID: {
   154→				ID:     channelID,
   155→				Name:   "test-webhook",
   156→				Type:   "webhook",
   157→				Config: json.RawMessage(`{"url":"http://example.com/hook"}`),
   158→			},
   159→		},
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	orch := &Orchestrator{store: store, llmConfig: LLMConfig{GoogleAPIKey: "test-key"}}
   163→
   164→	run := &models.AgentRun{ID: "run-1", ProjectID: projectID}
   165→	result := &agentResult{
   166→		semanticReleaseID: "sr-1",
   167→		version:           "v2.0.0",
   168→		reportText:        "Major release with breaking changes.",
   169→		projectName:       "My Project",
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	// sendProjectNotifications calls defaultSenders() which creates real HTTP
   173→	// senders. To test without making HTTP calls, we test that the store
   174→	// methods are called correctly. The real HTTP send will fail (no server),
   175→	// but that is logged and not returned as an error.
   176→	//
   177→	// To properly unit test the send path, we verify that
   178→	// ListProjectSubscriptions and GetChannel are called by checking that the
   179→	// method does not panic and the store state is accessed.
   180→	orch.sendProjectNotifications(context.Background(), run, result)
   181→
   182→	// Verify ListProjectSubscriptions was called (implicitly — if it returned
   183→	// subscriptions, GetChannel must have been called for the channel).
   184→	// The send itself will fail because there's no HTTP server, but that's
   185→	// expected and logged. The key assertion is that the method completes
   186→	// without error and accesses the store correctly.
   187→}
   188→
   189→func TestSendProjectNotifications_NoSubscriptions(t *testing.T) {
   190→	projectID := "proj-1"
   191→
   192→	store := &mockOrchestratorStore{
   193→		project:              &models.Project{ID: projectID, Name: "My Project"},
   194→		projectSubscriptions: []models.Subscription{}, // no subscriptions
   195→		channels:             map[string]*models.NotificationChannel{},
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	orch := &Orchestrator{store: store, llmConfig: LLMConfig{GoogleAPIKey: "test-key"}}

   323→func TestVersionPlaceholderSubstitution(t *testing.T) {
   324→	instruction := DefaultInstruction
   325→	if !strings.Contains(instruction, "{{VERSION}}") {
   326→		t.Fatal("DefaultInstruction must contain {{VERSION}} placeholder")
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	replaced := strings.ReplaceAll(instruction, "{{VERSION}}", "v1.10.15")
   330→	if strings.Contains(replaced, "{{VERSION}}") {
   331→		t.Fatal("replacement failed: still contains {{VERSION}}")
   332→	}
   333→	if !strings.Contains(replaced, "v1.10.15") {
   334→		t.Fatal("replacement failed: does not contain v1.10.15")
   335→	}
   336→}

   338→func TestCheckAllSourcesReady(t *testing.T) {
   339→	tests := []struct {
   340→		name     string
   341→		sources  []models.Source
   342→		hasMap   map[string]bool
   343→		version  string
   344→		expected bool
   345→	}{
   346→		{
   347→			name:     "no sources",
   348→			sources:  nil,
   349→			version:  "v1.0.0",
   350→			expected: true,
   351→		},
   352→		{
   353→			name: "all sources have version",
   354→			sources: []models.Source{
   355→				{ID: "s1"}, {ID: "s2"},
   356→			},
   357→			hasMap:   map[string]bool{"s1": true, "s2": true},
   358→			version:  "v1.0.0",
   359→			expected: true,
   360→		},
   361→		{
   362→			name: "one source missing",
   363→			sources: []models.Source{
   364→				{ID: "s1"}, {ID: "s2"},
   365→			},
   366→			hasMap:   map[string]bool{"s1": true, "s2": false},
   367→			version:  "v1.0.0",
   368→			expected: false,
   369→		},
   370→	}
   371→
   372→	for _, tt := range tests {
   373→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   374→			store := &mockOrchestratorStore{
   375→				sources:       tt.sources,
   376→				hasReleaseMap: tt.hasMap,
   377→			}
   378→			o := &Orchestrator{store: store}
   379→			got, err := o.checkAllSourcesReady(context.Background(), "proj-1", tt.version)
   380→			if err != nil {
   381→				t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   382→			}
   383→			if got != tt.expected {
   384→				t.Errorf("got %v, want %v", got, tt.expected)
   385→			}
   386→		})
   387→	}
   388→}

     1→package routing
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"net/url"
     8→	"strings"
     9→
    10→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// Notification is the payload sent through all notification channels.
    14→type Notification struct {
    15→	Title       string `json:"title"`
    16→	Body        string `json:"body"`
    17→	Version     string `json:"version"`
    18→	ProjectName string `json:"project_name,omitempty"`
    19→	Provider    string `json:"provider,omitempty"`
    20→	Repository  string `json:"repository,omitempty"`
    21→	ReleaseURL  string `json:"release_url,omitempty"`  // internal Changelogue link
    22→	SourceURL   string `json:"source_url,omitempty"`   // upstream provider link
    23→}
    24→
    25→// Sender is the interface that all notification channel implementations must satisfy.
    26→type Sender interface {
    27→	Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error
    28→}
    29→
    30→// rawBodyFields contains the human-readable fields extracted from raw release JSON.
    31→type rawBodyFields struct {
    32→	Changelog  string
    33→	ReleaseURL string
    34→}
    35→
    36→// parseRawBody extracts known fields from a raw JSON release body.
    37→func parseRawBody(body string) (rawBodyFields, bool) {
    38→	var raw map[string]interface{}
    39→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(body), &raw); err != nil {
    40→		return rawBodyFields{}, false
    41→	}
    42→
    43→	fields := rawBodyFields{
    44→		Changelog:  stringField(raw, "changelog"),
    45→		ReleaseURL: stringField(raw, "release_url"),
    46→	}
    47→
    48→	if fields.Changelog == "" && fields.ReleaseURL == "" {
    49→		return fields, false
    50→	}
    51→	return fields, true
    52→}
    53→
    54→func stringField(m map[string]interface{}, key string) string {
    55→	v, _ := m[key].(string)
    56→	return v
    57→}
    58→
    59→// ProviderURL builds the upstream release page URL for a given provider.
    60→func ProviderURL(provider, repository, version string) string {
    61→	switch strings.ToLower(provider) {
    62→	case "github":
    63→		return fmt.Sprintf("https://github.com/%s/releases/tag/%s", repository, version)
    64→	case "dockerhub":
    65→		return fmt.Sprintf("https://hub.docker.com/r/%s/tags?name=%s", repository, url.QueryEscape(version))
    66→	case "ecr-public":
    67→		return fmt.Sprintf("https://gallery.ecr.aws/%s", repository)
    68→	case "gitlab":
    69→		return fmt.Sprintf("https://gitlab.com/%s/-/releases/%s", repository, version)
    70→	default:
    71→		return ""
    72→	}
    73→}
    74→
    75→// ProviderLabel returns a human-readable label for a provider.
    76→func ProviderLabel(provider string) string {
    77→	switch strings.ToLower(provider) {
    78→	case "github":
    79→		return "GitHub"
    80→	case "dockerhub":
    81→		return "Docker Hub"
    82→	case "ecr-public":
    83→		return "ECR Public"
    84→	case "gitlab":
    85→		return "GitLab"
    86→	default:
    87→		return provider
    88→	}
    89→}
    90→

    80→// same agent configuration is used everywhere.
    81→func BuildAgent(ctx context.Context, store AgentDataStore, project *models.Project, llmConfig LLMConfig, version string) (agent.Agent, error) {
    82→	instruction := DefaultInstruction
    83→	if project.AgentPrompt != "" {
    84→		instruction = project.AgentPrompt + "\n\n" + instruction
    85→	}
    86→	instruction = strings.ReplaceAll(instruction, "{{VERSION}}", version)
    87→
    88→	llmModel, err := NewLLMModel(ctx, llmConfig)
    89→	if err != nil {
    90→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create LLM model: %w", err)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	// Create project-scoped function tools.
    94→	functionTools, err := NewTools(store, project.ID)
    95→	if err != nil {
    96→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create agent tools: %w", err)
    97→	}
    98→
    99→	// Data sub-agent: handles DB queries for releases and context sources.
   100→	dataAgent, err := llmagent.New(llmagent.Config{
   101→		Name:        "data_agent",
   102→		Description: "Query project releases and context sources from the database. Use this to fetch release lists, release details, and context sources like runbooks and documentation.",
   103→		Model:       llmModel,
   104→		Tools:       functionTools,
   105→	})
   106→	if err != nil {
   107→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create data sub-agent: %w", err)
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	// Search sub-agent: provider-specific web search.
   111→	var searchTool tool.Tool
   112→	var searchModel model.LLM
   113→	switch llmConfig.Provider {
   114→	case "openai":
   115→		searchTool = oaimodel.WebSearch{}
   116→		// OpenAI web search requires a search-capable model.
   117→		searchModelName := llmConfig.OpenAISearchModel
   118→		if searchModelName == "" {
   119→			searchModelName = "gpt-5-search-api"
   120→		}
   121→		searchModel, err = NewLLMModel(ctx, LLMConfig{
   122→			Provider:      "openai",
   123→			Model:         searchModelName,
   124→			OpenAIAPIKey:  llmConfig.OpenAIAPIKey,
   125→			OpenAIBaseURL: llmConfig.OpenAIBaseURL,
   126→		})
   127→		if err != nil {
   128→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("create OpenAI search model: %w", err)
   129→		}
   130→	default: // gemini
   131→		searchTool = geminitool.GoogleSearch{}
   132→		searchModel = llmModel
   133→	}
   134→
   135→	searchAgent, err := llmagent.New(llmagent.Config{
   136→		Name:        "search_agent",
   137→		Description: "Search the web for additional context about a release. Use this ONLY when you need information not available from the project's sources, such as community sentiment, security advisories, network adoption statistics, or known issues.",
   138→		Model:       searchModel,
   139→		Tools:       []tool.Tool{searchTool},
   140→	})
   141→	if err != nil {
   142→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create search sub-agent: %w", err)
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	// Root agent orchestrates data lookup and web search via sub-agents.
   146→	return llmagent.New(llmagent.Config{
   147→		Name:        "release_analyst",
   148→		Description: "Analyzes upstream releases and produces semantic release reports.",
   149→		Model:       llmModel,
   150→		Instruction: instruction,
   151→		Tools: []tool.Tool{
   152→			agenttool.New(dataAgent, nil),
   153→			agenttool.New(searchAgent, nil),
   154→		},
   155→	})
   156→}
   157→
   158→// checkAllSourcesReady returns true if every source in the project has a
   159→// release matching the target version.
   160→func (o *Orchestrator) checkAllSourcesReady(ctx context.Context, projectID, version string) (bool, error) {
   161→	// Use a high limit to fetch all sources; projects rarely exceed a handful.
   162→	sources, _, err := o.store.ListSourcesByProject(ctx, projectID, 1, 1000)
   163→	if err != nil {
   164→		return false, fmt.Errorf("list sources: %w", err)
   165→	}
   166→	if len(sources) == 0 {
   167→		return true, nil
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	for _, src := range sources {
   171→		has, err := o.store.HasReleaseForVersion(ctx, src.ID, version)
   172→		if err != nil {
   173→			return false, fmt.Errorf("check source %s: %w", src.ID, err)
   174→		}
   175→		if !has {
   176→			slog.Info("agent: source not ready for version",
   177→				"project_id", projectID,
   178→				"source_id", src.ID,
   179→				"version", version,
   180→			)
   181→			return false, nil
   182→		}
   183→	}
   184→	return true, nil
   185→}
   186→
   187→// Orchestrator manages the lifecycle of an agent run: loading project config,
   188→// creating an ADK-Go agent with project-specific tools and instructions,
   189→// running the agent, and persisting the result as a semantic release.
   190→type Orchestrator struct {
   191→	store     OrchestratorStore
   192→	llmConfig LLMConfig
   193→}
   194→
   195→// NewOrchestrator creates a new Orchestrator. It validates that the provided
   196→// LLMConfig has the required API key for the configured provider. If the key
   197→// is missing, it returns an error so the caller can degrade gracefully.
   198→func NewOrchestrator(store OrchestratorStore, cfg LLMConfig) (*Orchestrator, error) {
   199→	switch cfg.Provider {
   200→	case "openai":
   201→		if cfg.OpenAIAPIKey == "" {
   202→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("OPENAI_API_KEY is not set; agent orchestrator requires an LLM API key")
   203→		}
   204→	default: // gemini
   205→		if cfg.GoogleAPIKey == "" {
   206→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("GOOGLE_API_KEY is not set; agent orchestrator requires an LLM API key")
   207→		}
   208→	}
   209→	return &Orchestrator{
   210→		store:     store,
   211→		llmConfig: cfg,
   212→	}, nil
   213→}
   214→
   215→// RunAgent executes the full agent lifecycle for a given agent run:
   216→//  1. Marks the run as "running".
   217→//  2. Loads the project and builds the agent prompt.
   218→//  3. Creates an ADK-Go LLM agent with project-scoped tools.
   219→//  4. Runs the agent and captures its final text response.
   220→//  5. Parses the response into a SemanticReport.
   221→//  6. Creates a SemanticRelease and links it to the agent run.
   222→//  7. Marks the run as "completed" (or "failed" on error).
   223→func (o *Orchestrator) RunAgent(ctx context.Context, run *models.AgentRun) error {
   224→	// Mark as running.
   225→	if err := o.store.UpdateAgentRunStatus(ctx, run.ID, "running"); err != nil {
   226→		return fmt.Errorf("update agent run status to running: %w", err)
   227→	}
   228→
   229→	// Execute the agent; capture any error to mark the run as failed.
   230→	result, err := o.executeAgent(ctx, run)
   231→	if err != nil {
   232→		slog.Error("agent run failed", "run_id", run.ID, "err", err)
   233→		// Best-effort: mark the run as failed.
   234→		if statusErr := o.store.UpdateAgentRunStatus(ctx, run.ID, "failed"); statusErr != nil {
   235→			slog.Error("agent: failed to mark run as failed",
   236→				"run_id", run.ID, "status_err", statusErr)
   237→		}
   238→		return err
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	// Link the semantic release to the agent run and mark completed.
   242→	if err := o.store.UpdateAgentRunResult(ctx, run.ID, result.semanticReleaseID); err != nil {
   243→		return fmt.Errorf("update agent run result: %w", err)
   244→	}
   245→	if err := o.store.UpdateAgentRunStatus(ctx, run.ID, "completed"); err != nil {
   246→		return fmt.Errorf("update agent run status to completed: %w", err)
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	// Send project-level notifications (best-effort; errors are logged, not returned).
   250→	o.sendProjectNotifications(ctx, run, result)
   251→
   252→	return nil
   253→}
   254→
   255→// agentResult holds the output of a successful agent execution, used to
   256→// construct notifications after the semantic release is persisted.
   257→type agentResult struct {
   258→	semanticReleaseID string
   259→	version           string
   260→	reportText        string
   261→	projectName       string
   262→}
   263→
   264→// executeAgent performs the actual LLM agent interaction and returns the
   265→// agentResult containing the semantic release ID and metadata for notifications.
   266→func (o *Orchestrator) executeAgent(ctx context.Context, run *models.AgentRun) (*agentResult, error) {
   267→	// Load project.
   268→	slog.Info("agent: loading project", "run_id", run.ID, "project_id", run.ProjectID)
   269→	project, err := o.store.GetProject(ctx, run.ProjectID)
   270→	if err != nil {
   271→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("get project: %w", err)
   272→	}
   273→	slog.Info("agent: project loaded", "run_id", run.ID, "project", project.Name)
   274→
   275→	// Extract target version from the agent run.
   276→	version := run.Version
   277→	if version == "" {
   278→		// Fallback: parse from trigger "auto:version:v1.10.15"
   279→		if strings.HasPrefix(run.Trigger, "auto:version:") {
   280→			version = strings.TrimPrefix(run.Trigger, "auto:version:")
   281→		}
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	// Build the agent using the shared constructor.
   285→	slog.Info("agent: building agent", "run_id", run.ID,
   286→		"provider", o.llmConfig.Provider, "model", o.llmConfig.Model,
   287→		"version", version)
   288→	agentInstance, err := BuildAgent(ctx, o.store, project, o.llmConfig, version)
   289→	if err != nil {
   290→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("build agent: %w", err)
   291→	}
   292→
   293→	// Create in-memory session service and a new session.
   294→	sessionService := session.InMemoryService()
   295→	createResp, err := sessionService.Create(ctx, &session.CreateRequest{
   296→		AppName: "changelogue",
   297→		UserID:  "system",
   298→	})
   299→	if err != nil {
   300→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create session: %w", err)
   301→	}
   302→	sess := createResp.Session
   303→
   304→	// Create the runner with logging plugin for agent trace visibility.
   305→	r, err := runner.New(runner.Config{
   306→		AppName:        "changelogue",
   307→		Agent:          agentInstance,
   308→		SessionService: sessionService,
   309→		PluginConfig: runner.PluginConfig{
   310→			Plugins:      []*plugin.Plugin{loggingplugin.MustNew("agent_trace")},
   311→			CloseTimeout: 5 * time.Second,
   312→		},
   313→	})
   314→	if err != nil {
   315→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create runner: %w", err)
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	// Run the agent with a prompt requesting analysis.
   319→	slog.Info("agent: starting LLM run", "run_id", run.ID, "project", project.Name, "version", version)
   320→	var userPrompt string
   321→	if version != "" {
   322→		userPrompt = fmt.Sprintf("Analyze version %s for this project. Cross-check all sources and produce a semantic release report.", version)
   323→	} else {
   324→		userPrompt = "Analyze the recent releases for this project and produce a semantic release report."
   325→	}
   326→	userMsg := genai.NewContentFromText(userPrompt, "user")
   327→
   328→	var finalText string
   329→	eventCount := 0
   330→	for event, err := range r.Run(ctx, "system", sess.ID(), userMsg, agent.RunConfig{}) {
   331→		if err != nil {
   332→			slog.Error("agent: run event error",
   333→				"run_id", run.ID,
   334→				"event_count", eventCount,
   335→				"err", err,
   336→			)
   337→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent run event error: %w", err)
   338→		}
   339→		eventCount++
   340→		if event != nil && event.IsFinalResponse() && event.Content != nil {
   341→			for _, part := range event.Content.Parts {
   342→				if part.Text != "" {
   343→					finalText += part.Text
   344→				}
   345→			}
   346→		}
   347→	}
   348→	slog.Info("agent: LLM run finished",
   349→		"run_id", run.ID,
   350→		"event_count", eventCount,
   351→		"output_length", len(finalText),
   352→	)
   353→
   354→	if finalText == "" {
   355→		slog.Error("agent: produced no output",
   356→			"run_id", run.ID,
   357→			"project_id", run.ProjectID,
   358→			"event_count", eventCount,
   359→		)
   360→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent produced no output")
   361→	}
   362→
   363→	// Parse the agent's response into a SemanticReport.
   364→	report, err := parseReport(finalText)
   365→	if err != nil {
   366→		slog.Warn("agent output was not valid JSON report, storing raw",
   367→			"run_id", run.ID, "parse_err", err, "raw_output", finalText)
   368→		// Fall back to storing the raw text as the summary.
   369→		report = &models.SemanticReport{
   370→			Summary: finalText,
   371→		}
   372→	}
   373→
   374→	reportJSON, err := json.Marshal(report)
   375→	if err != nil {
   376→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("marshal report: %w", err)
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	// Gather release IDs for the semantic_release_sources join table.
   380→	// Only link releases that match the target version.
   381→	releases, _, err := o.store.ListReleasesByProject(ctx, run.ProjectID, 1, 200)
   382→	if err != nil {
   383→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list releases for semantic release: %w", err)
   384→	}
   385→	releaseIDs := make([]string, 0, len(releases))
   386→	for _, r := range releases {
   387→		if r.Version == version {
   388→			releaseIDs = append(releaseIDs, r.ID)
   389→		}
   390→	}
   391→
   392→	// Use the target version for the semantic release.
   393→	srVersion := version
   394→	if srVersion == "" {
   395→		srVersion = "unknown"
   396→		if len(releases) > 0 {
   397→			srVersion = releases[0].Version
   398→		}
   399→	}
   400→
   401→	now := time.Now()
   402→	sr := &models.SemanticRelease{
   403→		ProjectID:   run.ProjectID,
   404→		Version:     srVersion,
   405→		Report:      reportJSON,
   406→		Status:      "completed",
   407→		CompletedAt: &now,
   408→	}
   409→
   410→	if err := o.store.CreateSemanticRelease(ctx, sr, releaseIDs); err != nil {
   411→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create semantic release: %w", err)
   412→	}
   413→
   414→	slog.Info("agent run produced semantic release",
   415→		"run_id", run.ID,
   416→		"semantic_release_id", sr.ID,
   417→		"version", sr.Version,
   418→	)
   419→
   420→	return &agentResult{
   421→		semanticReleaseID: sr.ID,
   422→		version:           srVersion,
   423→		reportText:        finalText,
   424→		projectName:       project.Name,
   425→	}, nil
   426→}
   427→
   428→// defaultSenders returns a map of channel type to Sender for the supported
   429→// notification channel types (webhook, slack, discord).
   430→func defaultSenders() map[string]routing.Sender {
   431→	httpClient := &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}
   432→	return map[string]routing.Sender{
   433→		"webhook": &routing.WebhookSender{Client: httpClient},
   434→		"slack":   &routing.SlackSender{Client: httpClient},
   435→		"discord": &routing.DiscordSender{Client: httpClient},
   436→	}
   437→}
   438→
   439→// sendProjectNotifications looks up project-level subscriptions and sends a
   440→// notification to each channel with the semantic release report. This is
   441→// best-effort: errors are logged but do not propagate to the caller.
   442→func (o *Orchestrator) sendProjectNotifications(ctx context.Context, run *models.AgentRun, result *agentResult) {
   443→	subs, err := o.store.ListProjectSubscriptions(ctx, run.ProjectID)
   444→	if err != nil {
   445→		slog.Error("list project subscriptions for notification",
   446→			"project_id", run.ProjectID, "err", err)
   447→		return
   448→	}
   449→	if len(subs) == 0 {
   450→		slog.Debug("agent: no project subscriptions configured",
   451→			"project_id", run.ProjectID)
   452→		return
   453→	}
   454→
   455→	senders := defaultSenders()
   456→
   457→	msg := routing.Notification{
   458→		Title:   fmt.Sprintf("Semantic Release Report: %s %s", result.projectName, result.version),
   459→		Body:    result.reportText,
   460→		Version: result.version,
   461→	}
   462→
   463→	for _, sub := range subs {
   464→		ch, err := o.store.GetChannel(ctx, sub.ChannelID)
   465→		if err != nil {
   466→			slog.Error("get channel for project notification",
   467→				"channel_id", sub.ChannelID, "err", err)
   468→			continue
   469→		}
   470→
   471→		sender, ok := senders[ch.Type]
   472→		if !ok {
   473→			slog.Warn("unknown channel type for project notification", "type", ch.Type)
   474→			continue
   475→		}
   476→
   477→		if err := sender.Send(ctx, ch, msg); err != nil {
   478→			slog.Error("send project notification failed",
   479→				"channel", ch.Name, "type", ch.Type, "err", err)
   480→		} else {
   481→			slog.Info("project notification sent",
   482→				"channel", ch.Name, "project_id", run.ProjectID, "version", result.version)
   483→		}
   484→	}
   485→}
   486→
   487→// parseReport attempts to parse the agent's text output as a SemanticReport JSON.
   488→// It handles cases where the agent wraps the JSON in markdown code blocks.
   489→func parseReport(text string) (*models.SemanticReport, error) {
   490→	// Strip markdown code fences if present.
   491→	cleaned := strings.TrimSpace(text)
   492→	if strings.HasPrefix(cleaned, "```") {
   493→		// Remove opening fence (with optional language tag).
   494→		if idx := strings.Index(cleaned, "\n"); idx != -1 {
   495→			cleaned = cleaned[idx+1:]
   496→		}
   497→		// Remove closing fence.
   498→		if idx := strings.LastIndex(cleaned, "```"); idx != -1 {
   499→			cleaned = cleaned[:idx]
   500→		}
   501→		cleaned = strings.TrimSpace(cleaned)
   502→	}
   503→
   504→	var report models.SemanticReport
   505→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(cleaned), &report); err != nil {
   506→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parse report JSON: %w", err)
   507→	}
   508→
   509→	if report.Subject == "" && report.Summary == "" {
   510→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("report is missing required 'subject' or 'summary' field")
   511→	}
   512→
   513→	return &report, nil
   514→}

   211→**Step 1: Rename riskEmoji to urgencyEmoji**
   212→
   213→The function is in `slack.go` but called from both `slack.go` and `discord.go`. Rename it and update the switch to handle both UPPERCASE (old risk_level format) and Capitalized (urgency format):
   214→
   215→```go
   216→// urgencyEmoji returns an emoji indicator for the given urgency level.
   217→func urgencyEmoji(level string) string {
   218→	switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(level)) {
   219→	case "LOW":
   220→		return "🟢"
   221→	case "MEDIUM":
   222→		return "🟡"
   223→	case "HIGH":
   224→		return "🔴"
   225→	case "CRITICAL":
   226→		return "⚫"
   227→	default:
   228→		return "⚪"
   229→	}
   230→}
   231→```

   250→Replace `TestSlackSender_SemanticReport` in `slack_test.go` with a test for the new compact format:
   251→
   252→```go
   253→func TestSlackSender_SemanticReport(t *testing.T) {
   254→	var received []byte
   255→	srv := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   256→		received, _ = io.ReadAll(r.Body)
   257→		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
   258→	}))
   259→	defer srv.Close()
   260→
   261→	sender := &SlackSender{Client: srv.Client()}
   262→	ch := &models.NotificationChannel{
   263→		Type:   "slack",
   264→		Config: json.RawMessage(`{"webhook_url": "` + srv.URL + `"}`),
   265→	}
   266→
   267→	reportJSON := `{
   268→		"subject": "Ready to Deploy: go-ethereum v1.16.4",
   269→		"urgency": "High",
   270→		"urgency_reason": "Security vulnerability patched",
   271→		"status_checks": ["Binaries Unverified", "Docker Image Unverified"],
   272→		"changelog_summary": "Security fixes and performance improvements",
   273→		"availability": "GA",
   274→		"adoption": "Recommended for production",
   275→		"recommendation": "Deploy after verifying checksums",
   276→		"download_commands": ["docker pull ethereum/client-go:v1.16.4"],
   277→		"download_links": ["https://github.com/ethereum/go-ethereum/releases/tag/v1.16.4"]
   278→	}`
   279→
   280→	msg := Notification{
   281→		Title:       "Semantic Release Report: go-ethereum v1.16.4",
   282→		Body:        reportJSON,
   283→		Version:     "v1.16.4",
   284→		ProjectName: "go-ethereum",
   285→		Provider:    "github",
   286→		Repository:  "ethereum/go-ethereum",
   287→		SourceURL:   "https://github.com/ethereum/go-ethereum/releases/tag/v1.16.4",
   288→		ReleaseURL:  "https://changelogue.example.com/sr/1",
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	err := sender.Send(context.Background(), ch, msg)
   292→	if err != nil {
   293→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	var payload slackPayload
   297→	if err := json.Unmarshal(received, &payload); err != nil {
   298→		t.Fatalf("received invalid JSON: %v", err)
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	// Compact format: header + urgency_reason (High) + changelog + download cmd + context = ~5 blocks
   302→	if len(payload.Blocks) < 3 || len(payload.Blocks) > 6 {
   303→		t.Fatalf("expected 3-6 blocks for compact semantic report, got %d", len(payload.Blocks))
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	// First block should be header containing project name, version, and urgency
   307→	if payload.Blocks[0].Type != "header" {
   308→		t.Fatalf("expected first block to be header, got %s", payload.Blocks[0].Type)
   309→	}
   310→	headerText := payload.Blocks[0].Text.Text
   311→	if !strings.Contains(headerText, "go-ethereum") || !strings.Contains(headerText, "v1.16.4") {
   312→		t.Fatalf("header should contain project name and version, got %q", headerText)
   313→	}
   314→	if !strings.Contains(headerText, "High") {
   315→		t.Fatalf("header should contain urgency level, got %q", headerText)
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	// Should NOT have a fields section (no separate risk/urgency/availability fields)
   319→	for _, b := range payload.Blocks {
   320→		if len(b.Fields) > 0 {
   321→			t.Fatal("compact format should not have field sections")
   322→		}
   323→	}
   324→
   325→	// Should have download command in a code block
   326→	hasCode := false
   327→	for _, b := range payload.Blocks {
   328→		if b.Text != nil && strings.Contains(b.Text.Text, "docker pull") {
   329→			hasCode = true
   330→		}
   331→	}
   332→	if !hasCode {
   333→		t.Fatal("expected download command")
   334→	}
   335→
   336→	// Last block should be context footer
   337→	lastBlock := payload.Blocks[len(payload.Blocks)-1]
   338→	if lastBlock.Type != "context" {
   339→		t.Fatalf("expected last block to be context, got %s", lastBlock.Type)
   340→	}
   341→}
   342→```

   403→**Step 3: Rewrite buildSemanticBlocks**
   404→
   405→Replace the entire `buildSemanticBlocks` function in `slack.go` with:
   406→
   407→```go
   408→// buildSemanticBlocks builds compact Slack Block Kit blocks from a SemanticReport.
   409→// For Critical/High urgency, includes the urgency reason. For Low/Medium, omits it.
   410→func buildSemanticBlocks(title string, report *models.SemanticReport, msg Notification) []slackBlock {
   411→	// Resolve urgency from new field or backward-compat risk_level
   412→	urgency := report.Urgency
   413→	if urgency == "" {
   414→		urgency = report.RiskLevel
   415→	}
   416→	urgencyReason := report.UrgencyReason
   417→	if urgencyReason == "" {
   418→		urgencyReason = report.RiskReason
   419→	}
   420→
   421→	// Header: "ProjectName vX.Y.Z — 🟢 Low Urgency"
   422→	headerText := title
   423→	if urgency != "" {
   424→		headerText = fmt.Sprintf("%s — %s %s Urgency", title, urgencyEmoji(urgency), urgency)
   425→	}
   426→	// Slack header max is 150 chars
   427→	if len(headerText) > 150 {
   428→		headerText = headerText[:147] + "..."
   429→	}
   430→
   431→	blocks := []slackBlock{
   432→		{Type: "header", Text: &slackText{Type: "plain_text", Text: headerText}},
   433→	}
   434→
   435→	// Urgency reason (only for Critical/High)
   436→	upperUrgency := strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(urgency))
   437→	if (upperUrgency == "CRITICAL" || upperUrgency == "HIGH") && urgencyReason != "" {
   438→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   439→			Type: "section",
   440→			Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("⚠️ %s", urgencyReason)},
   441→		})
   442→	}
   443→
   444→	// Changelog summary
   445→	summary := report.ChangelogSummary
   446→	if summary == "" {
   447→		summary = report.Summary
   448→	}
   449→	if summary != "" {
   450→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   451→			Type: "section",
   452→			Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: summary},
   453→		})
   454→	}
   455→
   456→	// First download command only
   457→	if len(report.DownloadCommands) > 0 {
   458→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   459→			Type: "section",
   460→			Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("`%s`", report.DownloadCommands[0])},
   461→		})
   462→	}
   463→
   464→	// Footer context with source info and links
   465→	var footerParts []string
   466→	if msg.Provider != "" && msg.Repository != "" {
   467→		footerParts = append(footerParts, fmt.Sprintf("%s · %s", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.Repository))
   468→	}
   469→	if msg.SourceURL != "" {
   470→		footerParts = append(footerParts, fmt.Sprintf("<%s|View on %s>", msg.SourceURL, ProviderLabel(msg.Provider)))
   471→	}
   472→	if msg.ReleaseURL != "" {
   473→		footerParts = append(footerParts, fmt.Sprintf("<%s|View in Changelogue>", msg.ReleaseURL))
   474→	}
   475→	if len(footerParts) > 0 {
   476→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   477→			Type: "context",
   478→			Elements: []slackText{
   479→				{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: strings.Join(footerParts, "  |  ")},
   480→			},
   481→		})
   482→	}
   483→
   484→	return blocks
   485→}
   486→```

   513→**Step 1: Add a Discord semantic report test**

   515→Add to `discord_test.go`:
   516→
   517→```go
   518→func TestDiscordSender_SemanticReport(t *testing.T) {
   519→	var received []byte
   520→	srv := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   521→		received, _ = io.ReadAll(r.Body)
   522→		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
   523→	}))
   524→	defer srv.Close()
   525→
   526→	sender := &DiscordSender{Client: srv.Client()}
   527→	ch := &models.NotificationChannel{
   528→		Type:   "discord",
   529→		Config: json.RawMessage(`{"webhook_url": "` + srv.URL + `"}`),
   530→	}
   531→
   532→	reportJSON := `{
   533→		"subject": "Ready to Deploy: go-ethereum v1.16.4",
   534→		"urgency": "Critical",
   535→		"urgency_reason": "Hard fork — deploy before block 18M",
   536→		"changelog_summary": "Consensus-critical update",
   537→		"download_commands": ["docker pull ethereum/client-go:v1.16.4"],
   538→		"availability": "GA"
   539→	}`
   540→
   541→	msg := Notification{
   542→		Title:       "go-ethereum v1.16.4",
   543→		Body:        reportJSON,
   544→		Version:     "v1.16.4",
   545→		ProjectName: "go-ethereum",
   546→		Provider:    "github",
   547→		Repository:  "ethereum/go-ethereum",
   548→		SourceURL:   "https://github.com/ethereum/go-ethereum/releases/tag/v1.16.4",
   549→		ReleaseURL:  "https://changelogue.example.com/sr/1",
   550→	}
   551→
   552→	err := sender.Send(context.Background(), ch, msg)
   553→	if err != nil {
   554→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   555→	}
   556→
   557→	var payload discordPayload
   558→	if err := json.Unmarshal(received, &payload); err != nil {
   559→		t.Fatalf("received invalid JSON: %v", err)
   560→	}
   561→
   562→	if len(payload.Embeds) != 1 {
   563→		t.Fatalf("expected 1 embed, got %d", len(payload.Embeds))
   564→	}
   565→
   566→	embed := payload.Embeds[0]
   567→
   568→	// Should contain urgency reason for Critical
   569→	if !strings.Contains(embed.Description, "Hard fork") {
   570→		t.Fatal("critical urgency should include urgency_reason in description")
   571→	}
   572→
   573→	// Should contain changelog
   574→	if !strings.Contains(embed.Description, "Consensus-critical update") {
   575→		t.Fatal("should include changelog summary")
   576→	}
   577→
   578→	// Should NOT have inline fields (compact format removes them)
   579→	if len(embed.Fields) > 0 {
   580→		t.Fatalf("compact format should not have embed fields, got %d", len(embed.Fields))
   581→	}
   582→
   583→	// Color should be CRITICAL near-black
   584→	if embed.Color != 0x111113 {
   585→		t.Fatalf("expected critical color 0x111113, got 0x%06X", embed.Color)
   586→	}
   587→}
   588→```

   595→**Step 3: Rename discordRiskColor to discordUrgencyColor and rewrite buildSemanticEmbed**
   596→
   597→Rename `discordRiskColor` to `discordUrgencyColor` (same logic, just rename):
   598→
   599→```go
   600→// discordUrgencyColor returns a Discord embed color based on urgency level.
   601→func discordUrgencyColor(level string) int {
   602→	switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(level)) {
   603→	case "CRITICAL":
   604→		return 0x111113 // near-black
   605→	case "HIGH":
   606→		return 0xDC2626 // red
   607→	case "MEDIUM":
   608→		return 0xD97706 // amber
   609→	case "LOW":
   610→		return 0x16A34A // green
   611→	default:
   612→		return 0x5865F2 // Discord blurple
   613→	}
   614→}
   615→```
   616→
   617→Rewrite `buildSemanticEmbed`:
   618→
   619→```go
   620→// buildSemanticEmbed builds a compact Discord embed from a SemanticReport.
   621→func buildSemanticEmbed(title string, version string, report *models.SemanticReport, msg Notification) discordEmbed {
   622→	// Resolve urgency from new field or backward-compat risk_level
   623→	urgency := report.Urgency
   624→	if urgency == "" {
   625→		urgency = report.RiskLevel
   626→	}
   627→	urgencyReason := report.UrgencyReason
   628→	if urgencyReason == "" {
   629→		urgencyReason = report.RiskReason
   630→	}
   631→
   632→	var descParts []string
   633→
   634→	// Urgency badge
   635→	if urgency != "" {
   636→		descParts = append(descParts, fmt.Sprintf("%s **%s Urgency**", urgencyEmoji(urgency), urgency))
   637→	}
   638→
   639→	// Urgency reason (only for Critical/High)
   640→	upperUrgency := strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(urgency))
   641→	if (upperUrgency == "CRITICAL" || upperUrgency == "HIGH") && urgencyReason != "" {
   642→		descParts = append(descParts, urgencyReason)
   643→	}
   644→
   645→	// Changelog summary
   646→	summary := report.ChangelogSummary
   647→	if summary == "" {
   648→		summary = report.Summary
   649→	}
   650→	if summary != "" {
   651→		descParts = append(descParts, summary)
   652→	}
   653→
   654→	// First download command
   655→	if len(report.DownloadCommands) > 0 {
   656→		descParts = append(descParts, fmt.Sprintf("`%s`", report.DownloadCommands[0]))
   657→	}
   658→
   659→	// Links
   660→	var linkParts []string
   661→	if msg.SourceURL != "" {
   662→		linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("[View on %s](%s)", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.SourceURL))
   663→	}
   664→	if msg.ReleaseURL != "" {
   665→		linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("[View in Changelogue](%s)", msg.ReleaseURL))
   666→	}
   667→	if len(linkParts) > 0 {
   668→		descParts = append(descParts, strings.Join(linkParts, "  •  "))
   669→	}
   670→
   671→	description := strings.Join(descParts, "\n\n")
   672→	if len(description) > discordEmbedDescriptionLimit {
   673→		description = description[:discordEmbedDescriptionLimit-3] + "..."
   674→	}
   675→
   676→	// Footer with source info
   677→	var footerText string
   678→	if msg.Provider != "" && msg.Repository != "" {
   679→		footerText = fmt.Sprintf("%s · %s", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.Repository)
   680→	}
   681→
   682→	embed := discordEmbed{
   683→		Title:       title,
   684→		Description: description,
   685→		Color:       discordUrgencyColor(urgency),
   686→	}
   687→	if footerText != "" {
   688→		embed.Footer = &discordEmbedFooter{Text: footerText}
   689→	}
   690→	return embed
   691→}
   692→```

     1→# Implementer Subagent Prompt Template
     2→
     3→Use this template when dispatching an implementer subagent.
     4→
     5→```
     6→Task tool (general-purpose):
     7→  description: "Implement Task N: [task name]"
     8→  prompt: |
     9→    You are implementing Task N: [task name]
    10→
    11→    ## Task Description
    12→
    13→    [FULL TEXT of task from plan - paste it here, don't make subagent read file]
    14→
    15→    ## Context
    16→
    17→    [Scene-setting: where this fits, dependencies, architectural context]
    18→
    19→    ## Before You Begin
    20→
    21→    If you have questions about:
    22→    - The requirements or acceptance criteria
    23→    - The approach or implementation strategy
    24→    - Dependencies or assumptions
    25→    - Anything unclear in the task description
    26→
    27→    **Ask them now.** Raise any concerns before starting work.
    28→
    29→    ## Your Job
    30→
    31→    Once you're clear on requirements:
    32→    1. Implement exactly what the task specifies
    33→    2. Write tests (following TDD if task says to)
    34→    3. Verify implementation works
    35→    4. Commit your work
    36→    5. Self-review (see below)
    37→    6. Report back
    38→
    39→    Work from: [directory]
    40→
    41→    **While you work:** If you encounter something unexpected or unclear, **ask questions**.
    42→    It's always OK to pause and clarify. Don't guess or make assumptions.
    43→
    44→    ## Before Reporting Back: Self-Review
    45→
    46→    Review your work with fresh eyes. Ask yourself:
    47→
    48→    **Completeness:**
    49→    - Did I fully implement everything in the spec?
    50→    - Did I miss any requirements?
    51→    - Are there edge cases I didn't handle?
    52→
    53→    **Quality:**
    54→    - Is this my best work?
    55→    - Are names clear and accurate (match what things do, not how they work)?
    56→    - Is the code clean and maintainable?
    57→
    58→    **Discipline:**
    59→    - Did I avoid overbuilding (YAGNI)?
    60→    - Did I only build what was requested?
    61→    - Did I follow existing patterns in the codebase?
    62→
    63→    **Testing:**
    64→    - Do tests actually verify behavior (not just mock behavior)?
    65→    - Did I follow TDD if required?
    66→    - Are tests comprehensive?
    67→
    68→    If you find issues during self-review, fix them now before reporting.
    69→
    70→    ## Report Format
    71→
    72→    When done, report:
    73→    - What you implemented
    74→    - What you tested and test results
    75→    - Files changed
    76→    - Self-review findings (if any)
    77→    - Any issues or concerns
    78→```

     1→# Spec Compliance Reviewer Prompt Template
     2→
     3→Use this template when dispatching a spec compliance reviewer subagent.
     4→
     5→**Purpose:** Verify implementer built what was requested (nothing more, nothing less)
     6→
     7→```
     8→Task tool (general-purpose):
     9→  description: "Review spec compliance for Task N"
    10→  prompt: |
    11→    You are reviewing whether an implementation matches its specification.
    12→
    13→    ## What Was Requested
    14→
    15→    [FULL TEXT of task requirements]
    16→
    17→    ## What Implementer Claims They Built
    18→
    19→    [From implementer's report]
    20→
    21→    ## CRITICAL: Do Not Trust the Report
    22→
    23→    The implementer finished suspiciously quickly. Their report may be incomplete,
    24→    inaccurate, or optimistic. You MUST verify everything independently.
    25→
    26→    **DO NOT:**
    27→    - Take their word for what they implemented
    28→    - Trust their claims about completeness
    29→    - Accept their interpretation of requirements
    30→
    31→    **DO:**
    32→    - Read the actual code they wrote
    33→    - Compare actual implementation to requirements line by line
    34→    - Check for missing pieces they claimed to implement
    35→    - Look for extra features they didn't mention
    36→
    37→    ## Your Job
    38→
    39→    Read the implementation code and verify:
    40→
    41→    **Missing requirements:**
    42→    - Did they implement everything that was requested?
    43→    - Are there requirements they skipped or missed?
    44→    - Did they claim something works but didn't actually implement it?
    45→
    46→    **Extra/unneeded work:**
    47→    - Did they build things that weren't requested?
    48→    - Did they over-engineer or add unnecessary features?
    49→    - Did they add "nice to haves" that weren't in spec?
    50→
    51→    **Misunderstandings:**
    52→    - Did they interpret requirements differently than intended?
    53→    - Did they solve the wrong problem?
    54→    - Did they implement the right feature but wrong way?
    55→
    56→    **Verify by reading code, not by trusting report.**
    57→
    58→    Report:
    59→    - ✅ Spec compliant (if everything matches after code inspection)
    60→    - ❌ Issues found: [list specifically what's missing or extra, with file:line references]
    61→```

## Summary: Task 1 -- Update SemanticReport struct

### Changes made

**`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/models/semantic_release.go`**

The `SemanticReport` struct was updated with three changes:

1. **Added `UrgencyReason` field** (`json:"urgency_reason,omitempty"`) -- new field to provide context for the urgency level.
2. **Moved `Urgency` up** next to `Subject` as a primary field, reflecting the shift from risk-based to urgency-based semantics.
3. **Marked `RiskLevel` and `RiskReason` as `omitempty`** and moved them to a backward-compat section. Old reports stored in the database JSONB column still have these fields, so they must remain deserializable, but new reports will no longer emit them when empty.

**`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/models/semantic_release_test.go`**

1. **Updated `TestSemanticReportJSON`** -- now uses `UrgencyReason` instead of `RiskLevel`/`RiskReason` in the test fixture, and adds assertions for `Urgency` and `UrgencyReason` round-trip serialization.
2. **Added `TestSemanticReportJSON_BackwardCompat`** -- verifies that old-format JSON containing `risk_level` and `risk_reason` can still be unmarshaled correctly into the struct.

### Verification

- `go test ./internal/models/... -v` -- all 5 tests PASS
- `go vet ./...` -- clean, no errors across the entire codebase
- Commit: `82b01c5` on branch `main`

Perfect! Now I have a complete picture. Let me compile my findings:

## Source-Level Release Notification System - Complete Data Flow

I've traced the full data flow from release detection to notification delivery. Here's what I found:

### 1. **Data Ingestion & Raw Release Capture**

**File**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/ingestion/source.go`

The `IngestionResult` struct is the fundamental data structure:

```go
type IngestionResult struct {
    Repository string
    RawVersion string
    Changelog  string
    Metadata   map[string]string
    Timestamp  time.Time
}
```

**Provider Examples**:

- **GitHub** (`internal/ingestion/github.go`): Stores the release body as `Changelog` and metadata includes:
  ```go
  Metadata: map[string]string{
      "release_url": rel.HTMLURL,
      "prerelease":  prerelease, // "true" or "false"
  }
  ```

- **Docker Hub** (`internal/ingestion/dockerhub.go`): No changelog available (empty string), only metadata with tag name and timestamp

- Similar patterns for GitLab, ECR Public (each stores what's available from the upstream API)

### 2. **RawData Serialization for Storage**

**File**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/ingestion/pgstore.go` (lines 34-46)

When a release is ingested, the `IngestRelease` method constructs the final raw data JSON:

```go
// Build raw_data from Metadata + Changelog so nothing is lost.
raw := make(map[string]string)
for k, v := range result.Metadata {
    raw[k] = v
}
if result.Changelog != "" {
    raw["changelog"] = result.Changelog
}
rawData, err := json.Marshal(raw)
```

So for a **GitHub release**, the stored `RawData` is:
```json
{
    "release_url": "https://github.com/user/repo/releases/tag/v1.0.0",
    "prerelease": "false",
    "changelog": "## Changes\n- Fixed bug X\n- Added feature Y"
}
```

For **Docker Hub**, it would be simpler (no changelog).

### 3. **Notification Construction in NotifyWorker**

**File**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/worker.go` (lines 131-142)

The `NotifyWorker` creates the `Notification` struct:

```go
msg := Notification{
    Title:       title,  // "ProjectName — New release: v1.0.0"
    Body:        string(release.RawData),  // ← JSON string of raw metadata + changelog
    Version:     release.Version,
    ProjectName: release.ProjectName,
    Provider:    release.Provider,
    Repository:  release.Repository,
    SourceURL:   ProviderURL(release.Provider, release.Repository, release.Version),
}
if w.publicURL != "" {
    msg.ReleaseURL = fmt.Sprintf("%s/releases/%s", w.publicURL, release.ID)
}
```

**Key point**: The `Body` is the **JSON-serialized RawData** string.

### 4. **Slack Notification Fallback Path**

**File**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/slack.go` (lines 135-184)

The Slack sender has two paths:

1. **Semantic Report Path** (lines 143-145): If `msg.Body` parses as a valid `SemanticReport` with `Subject` field, uses rich formatting via `buildSemanticBlocks`

2. **Fallback Path** (lines 146-184): For source-level releases (raw RawData):
   - Tries to parse `msg.Body` via `parseRawBody()` to extract known fields
   - If successful, extracts `changelog` field and renders it
   - Includes source info links in footer: GitHub/Docker Hub URL + Changelogue internal link

### 5. **Discord Notification Fallback Path**

**File**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/discord.go` (lines 139-188)

Similar two-path approach:

1. **Semantic Report Path**: Rich formatted embed with urgency colors/reason
2. **Fallback Path** (lines 150-188): 
   - Calls `parseRawBody()` to extract changelog
   - Builds Discord embed with the changelog in description
   - Adds version field
   - Includes footer with provider + repository
   - Discord description is capped at 4096 chars

### 6. **Raw Body Parser**

**File**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/sender.go` (lines 36-52)

```go
func parseRawBody(body string) (rawBodyFields, bool) {
    var raw map[string]interface{}
    if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(body), &raw); err != nil {
        return rawBodyFields{}, false
    }
    
    fields := rawBodyFields{
        Changelog:  stringField(raw, "changelog"),
        ReleaseURL: stringField(raw, "release_url"),
    }
    
    if fields.Changelog == "" && fields.ReleaseURL == "" {
        return fields, false
    }
    return fields, true
}
```

This extracts the `changelog` and `release_url` keys from the raw JSON object.

### 7. **Webhook Fallback Path**

**File**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/webhook.go` (lines 34-56)

For source-level releases, sends the entire `Notification` struct as JSON:

```go
else {
    payload = msg  // Sends the full Notification (Title, Body, Version, Provider, Repository, etc.)
}
```

The webhook receives the raw notification intact.

---

## Summary: Source-Level Release Notification Format

When a new release is detected (before agent analysis):

1. **Initial Data**: Release metadata from upstream API + release notes/body
2. **Storage**: Merged into JSON object with `changelog` and provider-specific fields
3. **Notification Payload**:
   - **Title**: `"ProjectName — New release: v1.0.0"`
   - **Body**: JSON-serialized object containing changelog and metadata
   - **Version, Provider, Repository**: Extracted from source
   - **SourceURL**: Upstream provider link (GitHub/Docker Hub tag URL)
   - **ReleaseURL**: Internal Changelogue link if available

4. **Slack/Discord Formatting**:
   - Parse the changelog field from raw JSON
   - Display with title, changelog text, and source links
   - No urgency/semantic analysis (that's only for semantic reports)

5. **Webhook**: Receives complete `Notification` JSON with all fields

This is the **pure source-level notification** path—it fires immediately when a release is detected, before any LLM agent analysis produces a semantic report.

    26→// discordPayload is the Discord webhook payload.
    27→type discordPayload struct {
    28→	Embeds []discordEmbed `json:"embeds"`
    29→}
    30→
    31→type discordEmbed struct {
    32→	Title       string              `json:"title"`
    33→	URL         string              `json:"url,omitempty"`
    34→	Description string              `json:"description"`
    35→	Color       int                 `json:"color"`
    36→	Fields      []discordEmbedField `json:"fields,omitempty"`
    37→	Footer      *discordEmbedFooter `json:"footer,omitempty"`
    38→}
    39→
    40→type discordEmbedField struct {
    41→	Name   string `json:"name"`
    42→	Value  string `json:"value"`
    43→	Inline bool   `json:"inline"`
    44→}
    45→
    46→type discordEmbedFooter struct {
    47→	Text string `json:"text"`
    48→}
    49→
    50→// discordUrgencyColor returns a Discord embed color based on urgency level.
    51→func discordUrgencyColor(level string) int {
    52→	switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(level)) {
    53→	case "CRITICAL":
    54→		return 0x111113 // near-black
    55→	case "HIGH":
    56→		return 0xDC2626 // red
    57→	case "MEDIUM":
    58→		return 0xD97706 // amber
    59→	case "LOW":
    60→		return 0x16A34A // green
    61→	default:
    62→		return 0x5865F2 // Discord blurple
    63→	}
    64→}
    65→
    66→// buildSemanticEmbed builds a compact Discord embed from a SemanticReport.
    67→func buildSemanticEmbed(title string, version string, report *models.SemanticReport, msg Notification) discordEmbed {
    68→	// Resolve urgency from new field or backward-compat risk_level
    69→	urgency := report.Urgency
    70→	if urgency == "" {
    71→		urgency = report.RiskLevel
    72→	}
    73→	urgencyReason := report.UrgencyReason
    74→	if urgencyReason == "" {
    75→		urgencyReason = report.RiskReason
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	var descParts []string
    79→
    80→	// Urgency badge
    81→	if urgency != "" {
    82→		descParts = append(descParts, fmt.Sprintf("%s **%s Urgency**", urgencyEmoji(urgency), urgency))
    83→	}
    84→
    85→	// Urgency reason (only for Critical/High)
    86→	upperUrgency := strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(urgency))
    87→	if (upperUrgency == "CRITICAL" || upperUrgency == "HIGH") && urgencyReason != "" {
    88→		descParts = append(descParts, urgencyReason)
    89→	}
    90→
    91→	// Changelog summary
    92→	summary := report.ChangelogSummary
    93→	if summary == "" {
    94→		summary = report.Summary
    95→	}
    96→	if summary != "" {
    97→		descParts = append(descParts, summary)
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	// First download command
   101→	if len(report.DownloadCommands) > 0 {
   102→		descParts = append(descParts, fmt.Sprintf("`%s`", report.DownloadCommands[0]))
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	// Links
   106→	var linkParts []string
   107→	if msg.SourceURL != "" {
   108→		linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("[View on %s](%s)", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.SourceURL))
   109→	}
   110→	if msg.ReleaseURL != "" {
   111→		linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("[View in Changelogue](%s)", msg.ReleaseURL))
   112→	}
   113→	if len(linkParts) > 0 {
   114→		descParts = append(descParts, strings.Join(linkParts, "  •  "))
   115→	}
   116→
   117→	description := strings.Join(descParts, "\n\n")
   118→	if len(description) > discordEmbedDescriptionLimit {
   119→		description = description[:discordEmbedDescriptionLimit-3] + "..."
   120→	}
   121→
   122→	// Footer with source info
   123→	var footerText string
   124→	if msg.Provider != "" && msg.Repository != "" {
   125→		footerText = fmt.Sprintf("%s · %s", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.Repository)
   126→	}
   127→
   128→	embed := discordEmbed{
   129→		Title:       title,
   130→		Description: description,
   131→		Color:       discordUrgencyColor(urgency),
   132→	}
   133→	if footerText != "" {
   134→		embed.Footer = &discordEmbedFooter{Text: footerText}
   135→	}
   136→	return embed
   137→}
   138→
   139→func (s *DiscordSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error {
   140→	var cfg discordConfig
   141→	if err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &cfg); err != nil {
   142→		return fmt.Errorf("parse discord config: %w", err)
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	var embed discordEmbed
   146→	var report models.SemanticReport
   147→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(msg.Body), &report); err == nil && report.Subject != "" {
   148→		embed = buildSemanticEmbed(msg.Title, msg.Version, &report, msg)
   149→	} else {
   150→		// Fallback: extract known fields from raw data for a readable message.
   151→		var descParts []string
   152→		if fields, ok := parseRawBody(msg.Body); ok {
   153→			if fields.Changelog != "" {
   154→				descParts = append(descParts, fields.Changelog)
   155→			}
   156→		} else {
   157→			descParts = append(descParts, msg.Body)
   158→		}
   159→
   160→		// Add links
   161→		var linkParts []string
   162→		if msg.SourceURL != "" {
   163→			linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("[View on %s](%s)", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.SourceURL))
   164→		}
   165→		if msg.ReleaseURL != "" {
   166→			linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("[View in Changelogue](%s)", msg.ReleaseURL))
   167→		}
   168→		if len(linkParts) > 0 {
   169→			descParts = append(descParts, strings.Join(linkParts, "  •  "))
   170→		}
   171→
   172→		description := strings.Join(descParts, "\n\n")
   173→		if len(description) > discordEmbedDescriptionLimit {
   174→			description = description[:discordEmbedDescriptionLimit-3] + "..."
   175→		}
   176→
   177→		embed = discordEmbed{
   178→			Title:       msg.Title,
   179→			Description: description,
   180→			Color:       0x5865F2,
   181→			Fields: []discordEmbedField{
   182→				{Name: "Version", Value: msg.Version, Inline: true},
   183→			},
   184→		}
   185→		if msg.Provider != "" && msg.Repository != "" {
   186→			embed.Footer = &discordEmbedFooter{Text: fmt.Sprintf("%s · %s", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.Repository)}
   187→		}
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	payload := discordPayload{Embeds: []discordEmbed{embed}}
   191→
   192→	body, err := json.Marshal(payload)
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		return fmt.Errorf("marshal discord payload: %w", err)
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, cfg.WebhookURL, bytes.NewReader(body))
   198→	if err != nil {
   199→		return fmt.Errorf("create request: %w", err)
   200→	}
   201→	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   202→
   203→	client := s.Client
   204→	if client == nil {
   205→		client = http.DefaultClient
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   209→	if err != nil {
   210→		return fmt.Errorf("send discord notification: %w", err)
   211→	}
   212→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   213→
   214→	if resp.StatusCode >= 400 {
   215→		return fmt.Errorf("discord returned status %d", resp.StatusCode)
   216→	}
   217→	return nil
   218→}
   219→

    40→// urgencyEmoji returns an emoji indicator for the given urgency level.
    41→func urgencyEmoji(level string) string {
    42→	switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(level)) {
    43→	case "LOW":
    44→		return "🟢"
    45→	case "MEDIUM":
    46→		return "🟡"
    47→	case "HIGH":
    48→		return "🔴"
    49→	case "CRITICAL":
    50→		return "⚫"
    51→	default:
    52→		return "⚪"
    53→	}
    54→}
    55→
    56→// buildSemanticBlocks builds compact Slack Block Kit blocks from a SemanticReport.
    57→// For Critical/High urgency, includes the urgency reason. For Low/Medium, omits it.
    58→func buildSemanticBlocks(title string, report *models.SemanticReport, msg Notification) []slackBlock {
    59→	// Resolve urgency from new field or backward-compat risk_level
    60→	urgency := report.Urgency
    61→	if urgency == "" {
    62→		urgency = report.RiskLevel
    63→	}
    64→	urgencyReason := report.UrgencyReason
    65→	if urgencyReason == "" {
    66→		urgencyReason = report.RiskReason
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	// Header: "ProjectName vX.Y.Z — 🟢 Low Urgency"
    70→	headerText := title
    71→	if urgency != "" {
    72→		headerText = fmt.Sprintf("%s — %s %s Urgency", title, urgencyEmoji(urgency), urgency)
    73→	}
    74→	// Slack header max is 150 chars
    75→	if len(headerText) > 150 {
    76→		headerText = headerText[:147] + "..."
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	blocks := []slackBlock{
    80→		{Type: "header", Text: &slackText{Type: "plain_text", Text: headerText}},
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	// Urgency reason (only for Critical/High)
    84→	upperUrgency := strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(urgency))
    85→	if (upperUrgency == "CRITICAL" || upperUrgency == "HIGH") && urgencyReason != "" {
    86→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
    87→			Type: "section",
    88→			Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("⚠️ %s", urgencyReason)},
    89→		})
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	// Changelog summary
    93→	summary := report.ChangelogSummary
    94→	if summary == "" {
    95→		summary = report.Summary
    96→	}
    97→	if summary != "" {
    98→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
    99→			Type: "section",
   100→			Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: summary},
   101→		})
   102→	}
   103→
   104→	// First download command only
   105→	if len(report.DownloadCommands) > 0 {
   106→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   107→			Type: "section",
   108→			Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("`%s`", report.DownloadCommands[0])},
   109→		})
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	// Footer context with source info and links
   113→	var footerParts []string
   114→	if msg.Provider != "" && msg.Repository != "" {
   115→		footerParts = append(footerParts, fmt.Sprintf("%s · %s", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.Repository))
   116→	}
   117→	if msg.SourceURL != "" {
   118→		footerParts = append(footerParts, fmt.Sprintf("<%s|View on %s>", msg.SourceURL, ProviderLabel(msg.Provider)))
   119→	}
   120→	if msg.ReleaseURL != "" {
   121→		footerParts = append(footerParts, fmt.Sprintf("<%s|View in Changelogue>", msg.ReleaseURL))
   122→	}
   123→	if len(footerParts) > 0 {
   124→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   125→			Type: "context",
   126→			Elements: []slackText{
   127→				{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: strings.Join(footerParts, "  |  ")},
   128→			},
   129→		})
   130→	}
   131→
   132→	return blocks
   133→}
   134→
   135→func (s *SlackSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error {
   136→	var cfg slackConfig
   137→	if err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &cfg); err != nil {

   145→		blocks = buildSemanticBlocks(msg.Title, &report, msg)

   158→		} else {

   160→				Type: "section",
   161→				Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: msg.Body},
   162→			})
   163→		}
   164→
   165→		// Add source info and links
   166→		var linkParts []string
   167→		if msg.Provider != "" && msg.Repository != "" {

     1→package routing
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"log/slog"
     8→	"net/http"
     9→	"regexp"
    10→	"strings"
    11→	"time"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/riverqueue/river"
    14→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
    15→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/queue"
    16→)
    17→
    18→// NotifyStore is the data access interface required by the notification worker.
    19→type NotifyStore interface {
    20→	GetRelease(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Release, error)
    21→	GetSource(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Source, error)
    22→	ListSourceSubscriptions(ctx context.Context, sourceID string) ([]models.Subscription, error)
    23→	GetChannel(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.NotificationChannel, error)
    24→	GetProject(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.Project, error)
    25→	GetPreviousRelease(ctx context.Context, sourceID string, beforeVersion string) (*models.Release, error)
    26→	EnqueueAgentRun(ctx context.Context, projectID, trigger, version string) error
    27→}
    28→
    29→// NotifyWorker is a River worker that processes NotifyJobArgs.
    30→// It looks up the release, finds all source-level subscriptions, resolves
    31→// the notification channel for each, and dispatches via the appropriate sender.
    32→// After sending notifications, it checks the project's agent rules and
    33→// auto-triggers an agent run if the version criteria are met.
    34→type NotifyWorker struct {
    35→	river.WorkerDefaults[queue.NotifyJobArgs]
    36→	store     NotifyStore
    37→	senders   map[string]Sender
    38→	publicURL string // base URL for internal Changelogue links (e.g. "https://changelogue.example.com")
    39→}
    40→
    41→// NewSenders returns the default sender map for webhook, Slack, and Discord.
    42→func NewSenders() map[string]Sender {
    43→	return map[string]Sender{
    44→		"webhook": &WebhookSender{Client: &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}},
    45→		"slack":   &SlackSender{Client: &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}},
    46→		"discord": &DiscordSender{Client: &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}},
    47→	}
    48→}
    49→
    50→// NewNotifyWorker creates a NotifyWorker with default senders for webhook, Slack, and Discord.
    51→func NewNotifyWorker(store NotifyStore, publicURL string) *NotifyWorker {
    52→	return &NotifyWorker{
    53→		store:     store,
    54→		senders:   NewSenders(),
    55→		publicURL: strings.TrimRight(publicURL, "/"),
    56→	}
    57→}
    58→
    59→// VersionPassesFilter returns true if the version string passes the source's
    60→// include/exclude regex filters. When both are nil the version always passes.
    61→func VersionPassesFilter(version string, include, exclude *string) bool {
    62→	if include != nil && *include != "" {
    63→		matched, err := regexp.MatchString(*include, version)
    64→		if err != nil || !matched {
    65→			return false
    66→		}
    67→	}
    68→	if exclude != nil && *exclude != "" {
    69→		matched, err := regexp.MatchString(*exclude, version)
    70→		if err != nil || matched {
    71→			return false
    72→		}
    73→	}
    74→	return true
    75→}
    76→
    77→// Work processes a notify job: fetches the release, resolves subscriptions,
    78→// sends notifications to all matching channels, and checks agent rules to
    79→// auto-trigger agent runs when version criteria are met.
    80→func (w *NotifyWorker) Work(ctx context.Context, job *river.Job[queue.NotifyJobArgs]) error {
    81→	release, err := w.store.GetRelease(ctx, job.Args.ReleaseID)
    82→	if err != nil {
    83→		return fmt.Errorf("get release: %w", err)
    84→	}
    85→
    86→	// Check source version filters — skip entirely if filtered out.
    87→	source, err := w.store.GetSource(ctx, job.Args.SourceID)
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		return fmt.Errorf("get source: %w", err)
    90→	}
    91→	if !VersionPassesFilter(release.Version, source.VersionFilterInclude, source.VersionFilterExclude) {
    92→		slog.Debug("release filtered by version filter", "version", release.Version, "source_id", job.Args.SourceID)
    93→		return nil
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	// Check exclude_prereleases — skip if source excludes prereleases and this is one.
    97→	if source.ExcludePrereleases {
    98→		var rawData map[string]interface{}
    99→		if err := json.Unmarshal(release.RawData, &rawData); err == nil {
   100→			if prerelease, _ := rawData["prerelease"].(string); prerelease == "true" {
   101→				slog.Debug("release filtered by exclude_prereleases", "version", release.Version, "source_id", job.Args.SourceID)
   102→				return nil
   103→			}
   104→		}
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	subs, err := w.store.ListSourceSubscriptions(ctx, job.Args.SourceID)
   108→	if err != nil {
   109→		return fmt.Errorf("list subscriptions: %w", err)
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	for _, sub := range subs {
   113→		ch, err := w.store.GetChannel(ctx, sub.ChannelID)
   114→		if err != nil {
   115→			slog.Error("get channel failed", "channel_id", sub.ChannelID, "err", err)
   116→			continue
   117→		}
   118→
   119→		sender, ok := w.senders[ch.Type]
   120→		if !ok {
   121→			slog.Warn("unknown channel type", "type", ch.Type)
   122→			continue
   123→		}
   124→
   125→		// Build title with project name if available.
   126→		title := fmt.Sprintf("New release: %s", release.Version)
   127→		if release.ProjectName != "" {
   128→			title = fmt.Sprintf("%s — New release: %s", release.ProjectName, release.Version)
   129→		}
   130→
   131→		msg := Notification{
   132→			Title:       title,
   133→			Body:        string(release.RawData),
   134→			Version:     release.Version,
   135→			ProjectName: release.ProjectName,
   136→			Provider:    release.Provider,
   137→			Repository:  release.Repository,
   138→			SourceURL:   ProviderURL(release.Provider, release.Repository, release.Version),
   139→		}
   140→		if w.publicURL != "" {
   141→			msg.ReleaseURL = fmt.Sprintf("%s/releases/%s", w.publicURL, release.ID)
   142→		}
   143→
   144→		if err := sender.Send(ctx, ch, msg); err != nil {
   145→			slog.Error("send notification failed", "channel", ch.Name, "err", err)
   146→		}
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	// Check agent rules and auto-trigger if criteria are met.
   150→	w.checkAgentRules(ctx, release, source)
   151→
   152→	return nil
   153→}
   154→
   155→// checkAgentRules evaluates the project's agent rules against the new release
   156→// and the previous release for this source. If any rule matches, an agent run
   157→// is enqueued. Errors are logged but do not fail the job — notification delivery
   158→// is the primary responsibility.
   159→func (w *NotifyWorker) checkAgentRules(ctx context.Context, release *models.Release, source *models.Source) {
   160→	project, err := w.store.GetProject(ctx, source.ProjectID)
   161→	if err != nil {
   162→		slog.Error("get project for agent rules", "project_id", source.ProjectID, "err", err)
   163→		return
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	// Parse the project's agent_rules JSON.
   167→	var rules models.AgentRules
   168→	if len(project.AgentRules) > 0 {
   169→		if err := json.Unmarshal(project.AgentRules, &rules); err != nil {
   170→			slog.Error("unmarshal agent rules", "project_id", project.ID, "err", err)
   171→			return
   172→		}
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	// Determine the previous version for comparison.
   176→	var previousVersion string
   177→	prev, err := w.store.GetPreviousRelease(ctx, source.ID, release.Version)
   178→	if err != nil {
   179→		slog.Error("get previous release for agent rules", "source_id", source.ID, "err", err)
   180→		return
   181→	}
   182→	if prev != nil {
   183→		previousVersion = prev.Version
   184→	}
   185→
   186→	if CheckAgentRules(&rules, release.Version, previousVersion) {
   187→		trigger := fmt.Sprintf("auto:version:%s", release.Version)
   188→		if err := w.store.EnqueueAgentRun(ctx, source.ProjectID, trigger, release.Version); err != nil {
   189→			slog.Error("enqueue agent run", "project_id", source.ProjectID, "err", err)
   190→		} else {
   191→			slog.Info("agent run triggered by version rules",
   192→				"project_id", source.ProjectID,
   193→				"version", release.Version,
   194→				"previous_version", previousVersion,
   195→			)
   196→		}
   197→	}
   198→}
   199→

     1→package routing
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"net/url"
     8→	"strings"
     9→
    10→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// Notification is the payload sent through all notification channels.
    14→type Notification struct {
    15→	Title       string `json:"title"`
    16→	Body        string `json:"body"`
    17→	Version     string `json:"version"`
    18→	ProjectName string `json:"project_name,omitempty"`
    19→	Provider    string `json:"provider,omitempty"`
    20→	Repository  string `json:"repository,omitempty"`
    21→	ReleaseURL  string `json:"release_url,omitempty"`  // internal Changelogue link
    22→	SourceURL   string `json:"source_url,omitempty"`   // upstream provider link
    23→}
    24→
    25→// Sender is the interface that all notification channel implementations must satisfy.
    26→type Sender interface {
    27→	Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error
    28→}
    29→
    30→// rawBodyFields contains the human-readable fields extracted from raw release JSON.
    31→type rawBodyFields struct {
    32→	Changelog  string
    33→	ReleaseURL string
    34→}
    35→
    36→// parseRawBody extracts known fields from a raw JSON release body.
    37→func parseRawBody(body string) (rawBodyFields, bool) {
    38→	var raw map[string]interface{}
    39→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(body), &raw); err != nil {
    40→		return rawBodyFields{}, false
    41→	}
    42→
    43→	fields := rawBodyFields{
    44→		Changelog:  stringField(raw, "changelog"),
    45→		ReleaseURL: stringField(raw, "release_url"),
    46→	}
    47→
    48→	if fields.Changelog == "" && fields.ReleaseURL == "" {
    49→		return fields, false
    50→	}
    51→	return fields, true
    52→}
    53→
    54→func stringField(m map[string]interface{}, key string) string {
    55→	v, _ := m[key].(string)
    56→	return v
    57→}
    58→
    59→// ProviderURL builds the upstream release page URL for a given provider.
    60→func ProviderURL(provider, repository, version string) string {
    61→	switch strings.ToLower(provider) {
    62→	case "github":
    63→		return fmt.Sprintf("https://github.com/%s/releases/tag/%s", repository, version)
    64→	case "dockerhub":
    65→		return fmt.Sprintf("https://hub.docker.com/r/%s/tags?name=%s", repository, url.QueryEscape(version))
    66→	case "ecr-public":
    67→		return fmt.Sprintf("https://gallery.ecr.aws/%s", repository)
    68→	case "gitlab":
    69→		return fmt.Sprintf("https://gitlab.com/%s/-/releases/%s", repository, version)
    70→	default:
    71→		return ""
    72→	}
    73→}
    74→
    75→// ProviderLabel returns a human-readable label for a provider.
    76→func ProviderLabel(provider string) string {
    77→	switch strings.ToLower(provider) {
    78→	case "github":
    79→		return "GitHub"
    80→	case "dockerhub":
    81→		return "Docker Hub"
    82→	case "ecr-public":
    83→		return "ECR Public"
    84→	case "gitlab":
    85→		return "GitLab"
    86→	default:
    87→		return provider
    88→	}
    89→}
    90→

1. Truncate the changelog to a few lines
2. Use a Slack code block (and Discord equivalent) for the truncated changelog
3. Add a "Show more" link pointing to the full release on the provider

This is a straightforward change — no brainstorming needed, just formatting logic in the fallback paths. Let me also check the existing tests.

    91→func TestSlackSender_SemanticReport(t *testing.T) {
    92→	var received []byte
    93→	srv := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    94→		received, _ = io.ReadAll(r.Body)
    95→		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
    96→	}))
    97→	defer srv.Close()
    98→
    99→	sender := &SlackSender{Client: srv.Client()}
   100→	ch := &models.NotificationChannel{
   101→		Type:   "slack",
   102→		Config: json.RawMessage(`{"webhook_url": "` + srv.URL + `"}`),
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	reportJSON := `{
   106→		"subject": "Ready to Deploy: go-ethereum v1.16.4",
   107→		"urgency": "High",
   108→		"urgency_reason": "Security vulnerability patched",
   109→		"status_checks": ["Binaries Unverified", "Docker Image Unverified"],
   110→		"changelog_summary": "Security fixes and performance improvements",
   111→		"availability": "GA",
   112→		"adoption": "Recommended for production",
   113→		"recommendation": "Deploy after verifying checksums",
   114→		"download_commands": ["docker pull ethereum/client-go:v1.16.4"],
   115→		"download_links": ["https://github.com/ethereum/go-ethereum/releases/tag/v1.16.4"]
   116→	}`
   117→
   118→	msg := Notification{
   119→		Title:       "Semantic Release Report: go-ethereum v1.16.4",
   120→		Body:        reportJSON,
   121→		Version:     "v1.16.4",
   122→		ProjectName: "go-ethereum",
   123→		Provider:    "github",
   124→		Repository:  "ethereum/go-ethereum",
   125→		SourceURL:   "https://github.com/ethereum/go-ethereum/releases/tag/v1.16.4",
   126→		ReleaseURL:  "https://changelogue.example.com/sr/1",
   127→	}
   128→
   129→	err := sender.Send(context.Background(), ch, msg)
   130→	if err != nil {
   131→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   132→	}
   133→
   134→	var payload slackPayload
   135→	if err := json.Unmarshal(received, &payload); err != nil {
   136→		t.Fatalf("received invalid JSON: %v", err)
   137→	}
   138→
   139→	// Compact format: header + urgency_reason (High) + changelog + download cmd + context = 5 blocks
   140→	if len(payload.Blocks) < 3 || len(payload.Blocks) > 6 {
   141→		t.Fatalf("expected 3-6 blocks for compact semantic report, got %d", len(payload.Blocks))
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	// First block should be header containing project name, version, and urgency
   145→	if payload.Blocks[0].Type != "header" {
   146→		t.Fatalf("expected first block to be header, got %s", payload.Blocks[0].Type)
   147→	}
   148→	headerText := payload.Blocks[0].Text.Text
   149→	if !strings.Contains(headerText, "go-ethereum") || !strings.Contains(headerText, "v1.16.4") {
   150→		t.Fatalf("header should contain project name and version, got %q", headerText)
   151→	}
   152→	if !strings.Contains(headerText, "High") {
   153→		t.Fatalf("header should contain urgency level, got %q", headerText)
   154→	}
   155→
   156→	// Should NOT have a fields section (no separate risk/urgency/availability fields)
   157→	for _, b := range payload.Blocks {
   158→		if len(b.Fields) > 0 {

   199→		"changelog_summary": "Bumped go-libp2p to v0.30.0",
   200→		"availability": "GA",
   201→		"download_commands": ["docker pull ethereum/client-go:v1.16.5"]
   202→	}`
   203→
   204→	msg := Notification{

   208→		ProjectName: "go-ethereum",
