   211→	}

   213→	err := sender.Send(context.Background(), ch, msg)
   214→	if err != nil {

   216→	}

   226→			t.Fatal("low urgency should not include urgency_reason in notification")
   227→		}
   228→	}
   229→}

   135→func (s *SlackSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error {
   136→	var cfg slackConfig
   137→	if err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &cfg); err != nil {
   138→		return fmt.Errorf("parse slack config: %w", err)
   139→	}
   140→
   141→	// Try to parse body as a SemanticReport for rich formatting.
   142→	var blocks []slackBlock
   143→	var report models.SemanticReport
   144→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(msg.Body), &report); err == nil && report.Subject != "" {
   145→		blocks = buildSemanticBlocks(msg.Title, &report, msg)
   146→	} else {
   147→		// Fallback: extract known fields from raw data for a readable message.
   148→		blocks = []slackBlock{
   149→			{Type: "header", Text: &slackText{Type: "plain_text", Text: msg.Title}},
   150→		}
   151→		if fields, ok := parseRawBody(msg.Body); ok {
   152→			if fields.Changelog != "" {
   153→				blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   154→					Type: "section",
   155→					Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fields.Changelog},
   156→				})
   157→			}
   158→		} else {
   159→			blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   160→				Type: "section",
   161→				Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: msg.Body},
   162→			})
   163→		}
   164→
   165→		// Add source info and links
   166→		var linkParts []string
   167→		if msg.Provider != "" && msg.Repository != "" {
   168→			linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("%s · %s", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.Repository))
   169→		}
   170→		if msg.SourceURL != "" {
   171→			linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("<%s|View on %s>", msg.SourceURL, ProviderLabel(msg.Provider)))
   172→		}
   173→		if msg.ReleaseURL != "" {
   174→			linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("<%s|View in Changelogue>", msg.ReleaseURL))
   175→		}
   176→		if len(linkParts) > 0 {
   177→			blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   178→				Type: "context",
   179→				Elements: []slackText{
   180→					{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: strings.Join(linkParts, "  |  ")},
   181→				},
   182→			})
   183→		}
   184→	}
   185→
   186→	payload := slackPayload{Blocks: blocks}
   187→
   188→	body, err := json.Marshal(payload)
   189→	if err != nil {
   190→		return fmt.Errorf("marshal slack payload: %w", err)
   191→	}
   192→
   193→	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, cfg.WebhookURL, bytes.NewReader(body))
   194→	if err != nil {
   195→		return fmt.Errorf("create request: %w", err)
   196→	}
   197→	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   198→
   199→	client := s.Client
   200→	if client == nil {
   201→		client = http.DefaultClient
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   205→	if err != nil {
   206→		return fmt.Errorf("send slack notification: %w", err)
   207→	}
   208→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   209→
   210→	if resp.StatusCode >= 400 {
   211→		return fmt.Errorf("slack returned status %d", resp.StatusCode)
   212→	}
   213→	return nil
   214→}

   139→func (s *DiscordSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error {
   140→	var cfg discordConfig
   141→	if err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &cfg); err != nil {
   142→		return fmt.Errorf("parse discord config: %w", err)
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	var embed discordEmbed
   146→	var report models.SemanticReport
   147→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(msg.Body), &report); err == nil && report.Subject != "" {
   148→		embed = buildSemanticEmbed(msg.Title, msg.Version, &report, msg)
   149→	} else {
   150→		// Fallback: extract known fields from raw data for a readable message.
   151→		var descParts []string
   152→		if fields, ok := parseRawBody(msg.Body); ok {
   153→			if fields.Changelog != "" {
   154→				descParts = append(descParts, fields.Changelog)
   155→			}
   156→		} else {
   157→			descParts = append(descParts, msg.Body)
   158→		}
   159→
   160→		// Add links
   161→		var linkParts []string
   162→		if msg.SourceURL != "" {
   163→			linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("[View on %s](%s)", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.SourceURL))
   164→		}
   165→		if msg.ReleaseURL != "" {
   166→			linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("[View in Changelogue](%s)", msg.ReleaseURL))
   167→		}
   168→		if len(linkParts) > 0 {
   169→			descParts = append(descParts, strings.Join(linkParts, "  •  "))
   170→		}
   171→
   172→		description := strings.Join(descParts, "\n\n")
   173→		if len(description) > discordEmbedDescriptionLimit {
   174→			description = description[:discordEmbedDescriptionLimit-3] + "..."
   175→		}
   176→
   177→		embed = discordEmbed{
   178→			Title:       msg.Title,
   179→			Description: description,
   180→			Color:       0x5865F2,
   181→			Fields: []discordEmbedField{
   182→				{Name: "Version", Value: msg.Version, Inline: true},
   183→			},
   184→		}
   185→		if msg.Provider != "" && msg.Repository != "" {
   186→			embed.Footer = &discordEmbedFooter{Text: fmt.Sprintf("%s · %s", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.Repository)}
   187→		}
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	payload := discordPayload{Embeds: []discordEmbed{embed}}
   191→
   192→	body, err := json.Marshal(payload)
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		return fmt.Errorf("marshal discord payload: %w", err)
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, cfg.WebhookURL, bytes.NewReader(body))
   198→	if err != nil {
   199→		return fmt.Errorf("create request: %w", err)
   200→	}
   201→	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   202→
   203→	client := s.Client
   204→	if client == nil {
   205→		client = http.DefaultClient
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   209→	if err != nil {
   210→		return fmt.Errorf("send discord notification: %w", err)
   211→	}
   212→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   213→
   214→	if resp.StatusCode >= 400 {
   215→		return fmt.Errorf("discord returned status %d", resp.StatusCode)
   216→	}
   217→	return nil
   218→}

  "file_path": "/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/sender.go",
  "old_string": "func stringField(m map[string]interface{}, key string) string {\n\tv, _ := m[key].(string)\n\treturn v\n}",
  "new_string": "func stringField(m map[string]interface{}, key string) string {\n\tv, _ := m[key].(string)\n\treturn v\n}\n\n// changelogMaxLines is the maximum number of lines to show in a source-level\n// release notification before truncating with a \"Show more\" link.\nconst changelogMaxLines = 5\n\n// truncateChangelog truncates text to maxLines. Returns the truncated text\n// and whether truncation occurred.\nfunc truncateChangelog(text string, maxLines int) (string, bool) {\n\tlines := strings.Split(text, \"\\n\")\n\tif len(lines) <= maxLines {\n\t\treturn text, false\n\t}\n\treturn strings.Join(lines[:maxLines], \"\\n\"), true\n}"
}

> AGENT

Clean. Here's a summary of the changes:

**Slack** (`internal/routing/slack.go`): Wrapped the changelog in a code block (`` ``` ```) in the source-level fallback path. Slack automatically collapses long code blocks with a built-in "Show more" button.

**Discord** (`internal/routing/discord.go`): Same approach — wrapped the changelog in a code block in the fallback path. Discord also auto-collapses long code blocks.

**Tests** (`internal/routing/slack_test.go`): Updated `TestSlackSender_RawJSONFallback` to verify the changelog is wrapped in a code block.

The change is minimal — just wrapping `fields.Changelog` in triple backticks in both senders, letting Slack and Discord handle the collapsible UI natively.

   452→	}

   457→		Title:   fmt.Sprintf("Semantic Release Report: %s %s", result.projectName, result.version),
   458→		Body:    result.reportText,

   466→				"channel_id", sub.ChannelID, "err", err)

   468→		}
   469→

   472→			slog.Warn("unknown channel type for project notification", "type", ch.Type)
   473→			continue
   474→		}
   475→
   476→		if err := sender.Send(ctx, ch, msg); err != nil {
   477→			slog.Error("send project notification failed",
   478→				"channel", ch.Name, "type", ch.Type, "err", err)
   479→		} else {
   480→			slog.Info("project notification sent",
   481→				"channel", ch.Name, "project_id", run.ProjectID, "version", result.version)
   482→		}
   483→	}
   484→}
   485→
   486→// parseReport attempts to parse the agent's text output as a SemanticReport JSON.
   487→// It handles cases where the agent wraps the JSON in markdown code blocks.
   488→func parseReport(text string) (*models.SemanticReport, error) {
   489→	// Strip markdown code fences if present.
   490→	cleaned := strings.TrimSpace(text)
   491→	if strings.HasPrefix(cleaned, "```") {
   492→		// Remove opening fence (with optional language tag).
   493→		if idx := strings.Index(cleaned, "\n"); idx != -1 {
   494→			cleaned = cleaned[idx+1:]
   495→		}
   496→		// Remove closing fence.
   497→		if idx := strings.LastIndex(cleaned, "```"); idx != -1 {
   498→			cleaned = cleaned[:idx]
   499→		}
   500→		cleaned = strings.TrimSpace(cleaned)
   501→	}
   502→
   503→	var report models.SemanticReport
   504→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(cleaned), &report); err != nil {
   505→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parse report JSON: %w", err)
   506→	}
   507→
   508→	if report.Subject == "" && report.Summary == "" {
   509→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("report is missing required 'subject' or 'summary' field")
   510→	}
   511→
   512→	return &report, nil
   513→}

> AGENT

Pushed. Here's what was fixed:

**Semantic release notifications were missing links.** The `sendProjectNotifications` in the orchestrator only set `Title`, `Body`, and `Version`. Now it also populates:
- `ProjectName` — from `result.projectName`
- `Provider` / `Repository` — looked up from the project's first source
- `SourceURL` — built via `ProviderURL()` (e.g. GitHub release page)
- `ReleaseURL` — built from `publicURL` pointing to `/projects/{id}/semantic-releases/{srId}`

**Source-level changelogs wrapped in code blocks.** Both Slack and Discord fallback paths now wrap `fields.Changelog` in triple backticks, letting the platforms auto-collapse long content with their built-in "Show more" button.

> DEVELOPER

Still not right Sending a Markdown Message (mrkdwn)
The modern and recommended way to send messages in Slack is by using Block Kit. You can create a section block and set the text object type to mrkdwn.

Here is a quick cheat sheet for Slack's mrkdwn syntax, as it differs slightly from standard Markdown:

Bold: *text* (Note: standard Markdown uses **)

Italics: _text_

Strikethrough: ~text~

Inline Code: `code`

Code Block: ```code```

Links: <https://example.com|Click Here> (Note: standard Markdown uses [text](url))

Lists: Use - or • at the start of a new line.

> DEVELOPER

continue

> AGENT

I see — Slack's `mrkdwn` doesn't support language-tagged code blocks like ` ```markdown `. The ` ```markdown ` tag would just render as literal text inside the code block. In Slack, code blocks are simply ` ```text``` ` with no language hint.

So for Slack, it should go back to plain ` ``` `. Discord does support language-tagged code blocks though.

> DEVELOPER

We should refer to this approach for slack auto-collapesed message:The Exact JSON Payload
To achieve a message that looks almost identical to your screenshot, you would send a payload like this to your webhook or chat.postMessage endpoint:

JSON
{
  "username": "NewReleases",
  "icon_emoji": ":robot_face:",
  "attachments": [
    {
      "color": "#D3D3D3", 
      "title": "moonbeam-foundation/moonbeam on GitHub",
      "title_link": "[https://github.com/moonbeam-foundation/moonbeam](https://github.com/moonbeam-foundation/moonbeam)",
      "mrkdwn_in": [
        "text"
      ],
      "text": "*Runtime runtime-4202* `runtime-4202`\n```--------\nRuntimes\n--------\n\nMoonbase\n\n(Add several more lines of text here...)\n(Line 7)\n(Line 8)\n(Line 9)\n(This extra length is what forces Slack to add the 'Show more' button!)```"
    }
  ]
}
A Quick Note on "Show more"
To guarantee that the "Show more" link appears, make sure the text you send is genuinely long enough. Usually, having more than 5 to 7 physical line breaks (\n) inside the attachment's text field is enough to trigger Slack's auto-collapse feature.

    18→// SlackSender sends notifications as Slack Block Kit messages via incoming webhook.
    19→type SlackSender struct {
    20→	Client *http.Client
    21→}
    22→
    23→// slackPayload is the Slack incoming webhook payload using Block Kit.
    24→type slackPayload struct {
    25→	Blocks []slackBlock `json:"blocks"`
    26→}
    27→
    28→type slackBlock struct {
    29→	Type     string      `json:"type"`
    30→	Text     *slackText  `json:"text,omitempty"`
    31→	Fields   []slackText `json:"fields,omitempty"`
    32→	Elements []slackText `json:"elements,omitempty"`
    33→}
    34→
    35→type slackText struct {
    36→	Type string `json:"type"`
    37→	Text string `json:"text"`
    38→}
    39→
    40→// urgencyEmoji returns an emoji indicator for the given urgency level.
    41→func urgencyEmoji(level string) string {
    42→	switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(level)) {
    43→	case "LOW":
    44→		return "🟢"
    45→	case "MEDIUM":
    46→		return "🟡"
    47→	case "HIGH":
    48→		return "🔴"
    49→	case "CRITICAL":
    50→		return "⚫"
    51→	default:
    52→		return "⚪"
    53→	}
    54→}
    55→
    56→// buildSemanticBlocks builds compact Slack Block Kit blocks from a SemanticReport.
    57→// For Critical/High urgency, includes the urgency reason. For Low/Medium, omits it.
    58→func buildSemanticBlocks(title string, report *models.SemanticReport, msg Notification) []slackBlock {
    59→	// Resolve urgency from new field or backward-compat risk_level
    60→	urgency := report.Urgency
    61→	if urgency == "" {
    62→		urgency = report.RiskLevel
    63→	}
    64→	urgencyReason := report.UrgencyReason
    65→	if urgencyReason == "" {
    66→		urgencyReason = report.RiskReason
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	// Header: "ProjectName vX.Y.Z — 🟢 Low Urgency"
    70→	headerText := title
    71→	if urgency != "" {
    72→		headerText = fmt.Sprintf("%s — %s %s Urgency", title, urgencyEmoji(urgency), urgency)
    73→	}
    74→	// Slack header max is 150 chars
    75→	if len(headerText) > 150 {
    76→		headerText = headerText[:147] + "..."
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	blocks := []slackBlock{
    80→		{Type: "header", Text: &slackText{Type: "plain_text", Text: headerText}},
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	// Urgency reason (only for Critical/High)
    84→	upperUrgency := strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(urgency))
    85→	if (upperUrgency == "CRITICAL" || upperUrgency == "HIGH") && urgencyReason != "" {
    86→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
    87→			Type: "section",
    88→			Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("⚠️ %s", urgencyReason)},
    89→		})
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	// Changelog summary
    93→	summary := report.ChangelogSummary
    94→	if summary == "" {
    95→		summary = report.Summary
    96→	}
    97→	if summary != "" {
    98→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
    99→			Type: "section",
   100→			Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: summary},
   101→		})
   102→	}
   103→
   104→	// First download command only
   105→	if len(report.DownloadCommands) > 0 {
   106→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   107→			Type: "section",
   108→			Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("`%s`", report.DownloadCommands[0])},
   109→		})
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	// Footer context with source info and links
   113→	var footerParts []string
   114→	if msg.Provider != "" && msg.Repository != "" {
   115→		footerParts = append(footerParts, fmt.Sprintf("%s · %s", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.Repository))
   116→	}
   117→	if msg.SourceURL != "" {
   118→		footerParts = append(footerParts, fmt.Sprintf("<%s|View on %s>", msg.SourceURL, ProviderLabel(msg.Provider)))
   119→	}
   120→	if msg.ReleaseURL != "" {
   121→		footerParts = append(footerParts, fmt.Sprintf("<%s|View in Changelogue>", msg.ReleaseURL))
   122→	}
   123→	if len(footerParts) > 0 {
   124→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   125→			Type: "context",
   126→			Elements: []slackText{
   127→				{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: strings.Join(footerParts, "  |  ")},
   128→			},
   129→		})
   130→	}
   131→
   132→	return blocks
   133→}
   134→
   135→func (s *SlackSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error {
   136→	var cfg slackConfig
   137→	if err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &cfg); err != nil {
   138→		return fmt.Errorf("parse slack config: %w", err)
   139→	}
   140→
   141→	// Try to parse body as a SemanticReport for rich formatting.
   142→	var blocks []slackBlock
   143→	var report models.SemanticReport
   144→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(msg.Body), &report); err == nil && report.Subject != "" {
   145→		blocks = buildSemanticBlocks(msg.Title, &report, msg)
   146→	} else {
   147→		// Fallback: extract known fields from raw data for a readable message.
   148→		blocks = []slackBlock{
   149→			{Type: "header", Text: &slackText{Type: "plain_text", Text: msg.Title}},
   150→		}
   151→		if fields, ok := parseRawBody(msg.Body); ok {
   152→			if fields.Changelog != "" {
   153→				// Wrap changelog in a code block — Slack auto-collapses long
   154→				// code blocks with a built-in "Show more" button.
   155→				blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   156→					Type: "section",
   157→					Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("```%s```", fields.Changelog)},
   158→				})
   159→			}
   160→		} else {
   161→			blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   162→				Type: "section",
   163→				Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: msg.Body},
   164→			})
   165→		}
   166→
   167→		// Add source info and links
   168→		var linkParts []string
   169→		if msg.Provider != "" && msg.Repository != "" {
   170→			linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("%s · %s", ProviderLabel(msg.Provider), msg.Repository))
   171→		}
   172→		if msg.SourceURL != "" {
   173→			linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("<%s|View on %s>", msg.SourceURL, ProviderLabel(msg.Provider)))
   174→		}
   175→		if msg.ReleaseURL != "" {
   176→			linkParts = append(linkParts, fmt.Sprintf("<%s|View in Changelogue>", msg.ReleaseURL))
   177→		}
   178→		if len(linkParts) > 0 {
   179→			blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   180→				Type: "context",
   181→				Elements: []slackText{
   182→					{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: strings.Join(linkParts, "  |  ")},
   183→				},
   184→			})
   185→		}
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	payload := slackPayload{Blocks: blocks}
   189→
   190→	body, err := json.Marshal(payload)
   191→	if err != nil {
   192→		return fmt.Errorf("marshal slack payload: %w", err)
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, cfg.WebhookURL, bytes.NewReader(body))
   196→	if err != nil {
   197→		return fmt.Errorf("create request: %w", err)
   198→	}
   199→	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   200→
   201→	client := s.Client
   202→	if client == nil {
   203→		client = http.DefaultClient
   204→	}
   205→
   206→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   207→	if err != nil {
   208→		return fmt.Errorf("send slack notification: %w", err)
   209→	}
   210→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   211→
   212→	if resp.StatusCode >= 400 {
   213→		return fmt.Errorf("slack returned status %d", resp.StatusCode)
   214→	}
   215→	return nil
   216→}

> AGENT

All green. Here's what changed:

- **Slack source-level notifications** now use the `attachments` API with a code block (```` ``` ````) for changelogs. Slack auto-collapses long attachment text with a "Show more" button. The attachment includes a clickable title (`{repo} on {provider}`), version badge, and the changelog in a code block.
- **Plain text fallback** (no changelog in body) still uses simple Block Kit blocks.
- **Test channel endpoint** (`POST /channels/{id}/test`) sends a realistic payload with a multi-line changelog, so it exercises the attachment+code block path.

Want me to commit and push?

> DEVELOPER

why the markdown is not properly rendererd, still show raw message?

> AGENT

You're right — Slack's `mrkdwn` inside code blocks (` ``` `) renders everything as plain text, so markdown formatting won't work inside code blocks. The code block is only meant for raw/preformatted text.

The changelog content from GitHub releases is markdown (headings, bold, links, lists etc.), and wrapping it in a code block strips all that formatting.

The solution is to put the changelog as regular `mrkdwn` text in the attachment (not in a code block). Slack's attachment auto-collapse "Show more" works on long text regardless — it doesn't require a code block. The code block was only shown in the reference example because that particular project happened to use it for preformatted content.

Let me fix this.

> DEVELOPER

No still not work, should refer to such payload:
{
  "username": "NewReleases",
  "icon_emoji": ":robot_face:",
  "attachments": [
    {
      "color": "#D3D3D3", 
      "title": "moonbeam-foundation/moonbeam on GitHub",
      "title_link": "[https://github.com/moonbeam-foundation/moonbeam](https://github.com/moonbeam-foundation/moonbeam)",
      "mrkdwn_in": [
        "text"
      ],
      "text": "*Runtime runtime-4202* `runtime-4202`\n```--------\nRuntimes\n--------\n\nMoonbase\n\n(Add several more lines of text here...)\n(Line 7)\n(Line 8)\n(Line 9)\n(This extra length is what forces Slack to add the 'Show more' button!)```"

> AGENT

I see — the reference payload does use a code block (` ``` `) inside the attachment text. The "Show more" is triggered by the length of text inside the code block in the attachment, not by mrkdwn rendering. The changelog is meant to be shown as preformatted text, and Slack collapses it when it's long enough.

The issue you're seeing ("raw message" / markdown not rendering) is likely because the GitHub changelog markdown contains characters like `*`, `_`, `#` that clash with Slack's `mrkdwn` when rendered outside a code block. Wrapping in a code block is the correct approach — it prevents broken formatting and triggers the auto-collapse.

Let me revert to the code block approach and make sure the payload matches the reference exactly.

can you transform the text before sending? parse the original GitHub Markdown and rewrite it into plain ASCII text before sending the JSON payload to Slack.

Here is exactly what that pre-processing script is doing and why it is necessary.

How the Text is Being Transformed
The bot is likely using Regular Expressions (Regex) or a Markdown parsing library to find specific patterns and replace them. Let's break down the exact changes:

Converting Headings to ASCII Art: * Original: ## Protocol

Transformation: The script detects the ##, removes it, measures the length of the word, and adds a matching line of dashes (--------) above and below it.

Parsing GitHub Pull Request Links:

Original: https://github.com/MystenLabs/sui/pull/25364

Transformation: The script uses Regex to look for GitHub PR URLs. It extracts the PR number at the very end (25364), and rewrites the string as #25364 ( <original_url> ).

Stripping Markdown Formatting:

Original: #### (Subheadings) and ` (Inline code like `114`)

Transformation: It completely deletes these characters. Since standard Markdown isn't fully supported inside Slack's code blocks, removing them makes the text cleaner.

    11→)
    12→
    13→// Notification is the payload sent through all notification channels.
    14→type Notification struct {
    15→	Title       string `json:"title"`
    16→	Body        string `json:"body"`
    17→	Version     string `json:"version"`
    18→	ProjectName string `json:"project_name,omitempty"`
    19→	Provider    string `json:"provider,omitempty"`
    20→	Repository  string `json:"repository,omitempty"`
    21→	ReleaseURL  string `json:"release_url,omitempty"`  // internal Changelogue link
    22→	SourceURL   string `json:"source_url,omitempty"`   // upstream provider link
    23→}
    24→
    25→// Sender is the interface that all notification channel implementations must satisfy.
    26→type Sender interface {
    27→	Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error
    28→}
    29→
    30→// rawBodyFields contains the human-readable fields extracted from raw release JSON.
    31→type rawBodyFields struct {
    32→	Changelog  string
    33→	ReleaseURL string
    34→}
    35→
    36→// parseRawBody extracts known fields from a raw JSON release body.
    37→func parseRawBody(body string) (rawBodyFields, bool) {
    38→	var raw map[string]interface{}
    39→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(body), &raw); err != nil {
    40→		return rawBodyFields{}, false
    41→	}
    42→
    43→	fields := rawBodyFields{
    44→		Changelog:  stringField(raw, "changelog"),
    45→		ReleaseURL: stringField(raw, "release_url"),
    46→	}
    47→
    48→	if fields.Changelog == "" && fields.ReleaseURL == "" {
    49→		return fields, false
    50→	}
    51→	return fields, true
    52→}
    53→
    54→func stringField(m map[string]interface{}, key string) string {
    55→	v, _ := m[key].(string)
    56→	return v
    57→}
    58→
    59→// ProviderURL builds the upstream release page URL for a given provider.
    60→func ProviderURL(provider, repository, version string) string {
    61→	switch strings.ToLower(provider) {
    62→	case "github":
    63→		return fmt.Sprintf("https://github.com/%s/releases/tag/%s", repository, version)
    64→	case "dockerhub":
    65→		return fmt.Sprintf("https://hub.docker.com/r/%s/tags?name=%s", repository, url.QueryEscape(version))
    66→	case "ecr-public":
    67→		return fmt.Sprintf("https://gallery.ecr.aws/%s", repository)
    68→	case "gitlab":
    69→		return fmt.Sprintf("https://gitlab.com/%s/-/releases/%s", repository, version)
    70→	default:
    71→		return ""
    72→	}
    73→}
    74→
    75→// ProviderLabel returns a human-readable label for a provider.
    76→func ProviderLabel(provider string) string {
    77→	switch strings.ToLower(provider) {

    80→	case "dockerhub":

    82→	case "ecr-public":
    83→		return "ECR Public"
    84→	case "gitlab":
    85→		return "GitLab"
    86→	default:
    87→		return provider
    88→	}
    89→}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/sender.go",
  "old_string": "// ProviderLabel returns a human-readable label for a provider.\nfunc ProviderLabel(provider string) string {\n\tswitch strings.ToLower(provider) {\n\tcase \"github\":\n\t\treturn \"GitHub\"\n\tcase \"dockerhub\":\n\t\treturn \"Docker Hub\"\n\tcase \"ecr-public\":\n\t\treturn \"ECR Public\"\n\tcase \"gitlab\":\n\t\treturn \"GitLab\"\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn provider\n\t}\n}",
  "new_string": "// ProviderLabel returns a human-readable label for a provider.\nfunc ProviderLabel(provider string) string {\n\tswitch strings.ToLower(provider) {\n\tcase \"github\":\n\t\treturn \"GitHub\"\n\tcase \"dockerhub\":\n\t\treturn \"Docker Hub\"\n\tcase \"ecr-public\":\n\t\treturn \"ECR Public\"\n\tcase \"gitlab\":\n\t\treturn \"GitLab\"\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn provider\n\t}\n}\n\n// Regex patterns for markdown-to-ASCII conversion.\nvar (\n\t// GitHub PR/issue URLs → #NNN ( url )\n\treGitHubPR = regexp.MustCompile(`https?://github\\.com/([^/]+/[^/]+)/(pull|issues)/(\\d+)`)\n\t// Markdown links [text](url) → text ( url )\n\treMdLink = regexp.MustCompile(`\\[([^\\]]+)\\]\\(([^)]+)\\)`)\n\t// Headings ## Text → dashed ASCII box\n\treHeading = regexp.MustCompile(`(?m)^(#{1,6})\\s+(.+)$`)\n\t// Bold **text** or __text__\n\treBold = regexp.MustCompile(`(\\*\\*|__)(.+?)\\1`)\n\t// Italic *text* or _text_ (but not inside words)\n\treItalic = regexp.MustCompile(`(?:^|[\\s(])([*_])([^*_]+?)\\1(?:[\\s).,;:!?]|$)`)\n\t// Inline code `text`\n\treInlineCode = regexp.MustCompile(\"`([^`]+)`\")\n\t// HTML tags\n\treHTML = regexp.MustCompile(`<[^>]+>`)\n\t// Images ![alt](url)\n\treImage = regexp.MustCompile(`!\\[([^\\]]*)\\]\\([^)]+\\)`)\n)\n\n// markdownToASCII converts GitHub-flavored Markdown into clean ASCII text\n// suitable for display inside Slack/Discord code blocks.\nfunc markdownToASCII(md string) string {\n\ts := md\n\n\t// Strip images first (before link processing)\n\ts = reImage.ReplaceAllString(s, \"$1\")\n\n\t// Convert GitHub PR/issue URLs to #NNN ( url )\n\ts = reGitHubPR.ReplaceAllString(s, \"#$3 ( $0 )\")\n\n\t// Convert markdown links [text](url) → text ( url )\n\ts = reMdLink.ReplaceAllString(s, \"$1 ( $2 )\")\n\n\t// Convert headings to ASCII art with dashes\n\ts = reHeading.ReplaceAllStringFunc(s, func(match string) string {\n\t\tparts := reHeading.FindStringSubmatch(match)\n\t\ttitle := strings.TrimSpace(parts[2])\n\t\tdashes := strings.Repeat(\"-\", len(title))\n\t\treturn dashes + \"\\n\" + title + \"\\n\" + dashes\n\t})\n\n\t// Strip bold markers\n\ts = reBold.ReplaceAllString(s, \"$2\")\n\n\t// Strip inline code backticks\n\ts = reInlineCode.ReplaceAllString(s, \"$1\")\n\n\t// Strip HTML tags\n\ts = reHTML.ReplaceAllString(s, \"\")\n\n\t// Collapse 3+ consecutive blank lines into 2\n\treBlankLines := regexp.MustCompile(`\\n{3,}`)\n\ts = reBlankLines.ReplaceAllString(s, \"\\n\\n\")\n\n\treturn strings.TrimSpace(s)\n}"
}

> AGENT

Go's `regexp` doesn't support backreferences (`\1`). Let me fix the patterns.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_vrtx_01Srm16SFDRBVX1bT868gvPC
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/sender.go",
  "old_string": "// Regex patterns for markdown-to-ASCII conversion.\nvar (\n\t// GitHub PR/issue URLs → #NNN ( url )\n\treGitHubPR = regexp.MustCompile(`https?://github\\.com/([^/]+/[^/]+)/(pull|issues)/(\\d+)`)\n\t// Markdown links [text](url) → text ( url )\n\treMdLink = regexp.MustCompile(`\\[([^\\]]+)\\]\\(([^)]+)\\)`)\n\t// Headings ## Text → dashed ASCII box\n\treHeading = regexp.MustCompile(`(?m)^(#{1,6})\\s+(.+)$`)\n\t// Bold **text** or __text__\n\treBold = regexp.MustCompile(`(\\*\\*|__)(.+?)\\1`)\n\t// Italic *text* or _text_ (but not inside words)\n\treItalic = regexp.MustCompile(`(?:^|[\\s(])([*_])([^*_]+?)\\1(?:[\\s).,;:!?]|$)`)\n\t// Inline code `text`\n\treInlineCode = regexp.MustCompile(\"`([^`]+)`\")\n\t// HTML tags\n\treHTML = regexp.MustCompile(`<[^>]+>`)\n\t// Images ![alt](url)\n\treImage = regexp.MustCompile(`!\\[([^\\]]*)\\]\\([^)]+\\)`)\n)",
  "new_string": "// Regex patterns for markdown-to-ASCII conversion.\nvar (\n\t// GitHub PR/issue URLs → #NNN ( url )\n\treGitHubPR = regexp.MustCompile(`https?://github\\.com/([^/]+/[^/]+)/(pull|issues)/(\\d+)`)\n\t// Markdown links [text](url) → text ( url )\n\treMdLink = regexp.MustCompile(`\\[([^\\]]+)\\]\\(([^)]+)\\)`)\n\t// Headings ## Text → dashed ASCII box\n\treHeading = regexp.MustCompile(`(?m)^#{1,6}\\s+(.+)$`)\n\t// Bold **text** and __text__\n\treBoldStar = regexp.MustCompile(`\\*\\*(.+?)\\*\\*`)\n\treBoldUnderscore = regexp.MustCompile(`__(.+?)__`)\n\t// Inline code `text`\n\treInlineCode = regexp.MustCompile(\"`([^`]+)`\")\n\t// HTML tags\n\treHTML = regexp.MustCompile(`<[^>]+>`)\n\t// Images ![alt](url)\n\treImage = regexp.MustCompile(`!\\[([^\\]]*)\\]\\([^)]+\\)`)\n)"
}
```
